     1→# Plan: Fix Structured Fields Mapping Panel
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→The structured fields modification panel in the HITL review UI is broken. Users see raw text instead of grounded SNOMED/UMLS concepts, relations and values are missing, and OMOP concept IDs are invisible. The root cause is a format mismatch between the AI pipeline output (`field_mapper.py`) and the frontend parser (`buildInitialValues` in `CriterionCard.tsx`), combined with key naming inconsistencies and missing OMOP display.
     6→
     7→5 root causes identified — see `INVESTIGATION_REPORT.md` in worktree root for full analysis.
     8→

     1→# Investigation Report: Structured Fields / Mapping Panel Issues
     2→
     3→## Executive Summary
     4→
     5→The "modification panel" (structured fields editor) shows **raw text instead of grounded SNOMED/UMLS concepts**, has **missing relations and values**, and has **no OMOP relation integration**. After a thorough investigation of the frontend, backend, DB schema, pipeline code, and MLFlow tracing, I identified **5 root causes** spanning the full stack. The core problem is a **format mismatch** between what the AI pipeline produces and what the frontend expects, compounded by the grounding pipeline not enriching field_mappings with concept codes.
     6→
     7→---
     8→
     9→## Issues Reported
    10→
    11→| # | Symptom | Severity |
    12→|---|---------|----------|
    13→| 1 | Failure to ground concepts with UMLS or SNOMED | High |
    14→| 2 | Raw text instead of defined concepts | High |
    15→| 3 | No relation or values in the modification panel | High |
    16→| 4 | No OMOP relations visible | Medium |
    17→
    18→---
    19→
    20→## Root Cause Analysis
    21→
    22→### ROOT CAUSE 1: Field Mapper Produces Flat Values, Frontend Expects Typed Objects
    23→
    24→**Location:**
    25→- Backend: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py:120-132`
    26→- Frontend: `apps/hitl-ui/src/components/CriterionCard.tsx:194-232`
    27→
    28→**Problem:**
    29→
    30→The Gemini-powered `field_mapper.py` produces field_mappings like:
    31→
    32→```json
    33→{
    34→  "entity": "Abstinence duration",
    35→  "relation": ">=",
    36→  "value": "6",       // <-- FLAT STRING
    37→  "unit": "months"    // <-- SEPARATE FIELD
    38→}
    39→```
    40→
    41→But the frontend's `buildInitialValues()` (Priority 1 path, line 197-231) expects a **typed value object**:
    42→
    43→```json
    44→{
    45→  "entity": "Abstinence duration",
    46→  "relation": ">=",
    47→  "value": {                        // <-- OBJECT with type discriminator
    48→    "type": "standard",
    49→    "value": "6",
    50→    "unit": "months"
    51→  }
    52→}
    53→```
    54→
    55→When `buildInitialValues` reads the stored field_mappings, it checks `rawVal.type` (line 203). Since `value` is a flat string `"6"` (not an object), all three branches (`range`, `temporal`, `standard`) fail, and `value` defaults to `{ type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' }` — **empty**.
    56→
    57→**Impact:** All AI-generated field mappings lose their **value and unit** when displayed in the modification panel.
    58→
    59→---
    60→
    61→### ROOT CAUSE 2: Relation Operators Don't Match Frontend Enum
    62→
    63→**Location:**
    64→- Backend: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py:106-107` (prompt instructs Gemini to use `<, >, >=, <=, =, has, is, within, not`)
    65→- Frontend: `apps/hitl-ui/src/components/structured-editor/constants.ts:12-27` (accepts `=, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within, not_in_last, contains, not_contains`)
    66→
    67→**Problem:**
    68→
    69→The field mapper prompt tells Gemini to emit relations like `has`, `is`, `not`. But the frontend's `RelationOperator` type only accepts: `=`, `!=`, `>`, `>=`, `<`, `<=`, `within`, `not_in_last`, `contains`, `not_contains`.
    70→
    71→When the backend produces `"relation": "has"` or `"relation": "is"`, the frontend casts it to `RelationOperator | ''` at line 200/227. Since `"has"` is not a valid `RelationOperator`, it falls through to an empty string `''`, meaning:
    72→
    73→1. The RelationSelect dropdown shows "Select relation..." (empty)
    74→2. The ValueInput is **conditionally hidden** (line 234: `{relationCategory && (...)}`) — if relation is empty, no value/unit inputs render at all
    75→
    76→**Impact:** Entities with `has`/`is`/`not` relations show as **no relation selected** and **no value fields**.
    77→
    78→---
    79→
    80→### ROOT CAUSE 3: Entity Grounding Codes Not Propagated to Stored field_mappings
    81→
    82→**Location:**
    83→- Backend: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py:120-131`
    84→- Backend: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/persist.py:162-174`
    85→
    86→**Problem:**
    87→
    88→The field_mapper does include `entity_concept_id`, `entity_concept_system`, and `omop_concept_id` in its output (lines 126-129). However, there are two failure modes:
    89→
    90→1. **Grounding confidence < 0.5**: The agentic retry loop may fail after 3 attempts, leaving `entity.selected_code = None` and `entity.selected_system = None`. The field_mapper then produces `entity_concept_id: null` and `entity_concept_system: null`.
    91→
    92→2. **field_mappings key naming mismatch**: The field_mapper stores `entity_concept_id` and `entity_concept_system`, but the frontend's `buildInitialValues` reads `entity_code` and `entity_system` (lines 225-226):
    93→   ```typescript
    94→   entity_code: fm.entity_code ? String(fm.entity_code) : undefined,
    95→   entity_system: fm.entity_system ? String(fm.entity_system) : undefined,
    96→   ```
    97→
    98→   So even when grounding succeeds, the concept codes stored as `entity_concept_id`/`entity_concept_system` are **never read** by the frontend because it looks for `entity_code`/`entity_system`.
    99→
   100→**Impact:** The green "SNOMED: 418633004" badge **never appears** for AI-generated mappings. Entities show as raw text only.
   101→
   102→---
   103→
   104→### ROOT CAUSE 4: OMOP Concept IDs Are Stored But Never Displayed
   105→
   106→**Location:**
   107→- Backend: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py:128` (stores `omop_concept_id`)
   108→- Frontend: `apps/hitl-ui/src/components/structured-editor/types.ts:41-47` (no `omop_concept_id` field)
   109→- Frontend: `apps/hitl-ui/src/components/FieldMappingBadges.tsx` (no OMOP rendering)
   110→
   111→**Problem:**
   112→
   113→The field_mapper correctly stores `omop_concept_id` in each mapping dict. But:
   114→1. The frontend `FieldMapping` TypeScript interface has no `omop_concept_id` field
   115→2. `FieldMappingBadges` only renders `entity_code` + `entity_system` as a badge
   116→3. There is no UI component that shows OMOP concept IDs or OMOP vocabulary relationships
   117→4. The `AtomicCriterion` table has `omop_concept_id`, `unit_concept_id`, and `value_concept_id` columns, but these are **never served to the frontend** in the API response
   118→
   119→**Impact:** OMOP concepts are invisible in the UI even when they exist in the database.
   120→
   121→---
   122→
   123→### ROOT CAUSE 5: buildInitialValues Priority 2 Path Has Entity-Threshold Index Mismatch
   124→
   125→**Location:**
   126→- Frontend: `apps/hitl-ui/src/components/CriterionCard.tsx:250-286`
   127→
   128→**Problem:**
   129→
   130→When there are no stored field_mappings (Priority 1 fails), the code falls back to Priority 2: inferring mappings from `criterion.entities` + `criterion.numeric_thresholds`. It matches entities to thresholds **by array index** (line 259):
   131→
   132→```typescript
   133→const matchedEntity = measurableEntities[i];
   134→```
   135→
   136→This assumes the i-th threshold corresponds to the i-th measurable entity (Lab_Value/Biomarker/Demographic). If the extraction pipeline produces 3 entities but only 1 threshold, or if entities and thresholds are in different orders, the match is wrong or missing.
   137→
   138→Additionally, non-measurable entities (Condition, Medication, Procedure) at lines 288-303 are added with **empty relation and empty value** — they appear as raw text labels with no structured data.
   139→
   140→**Impact:** Fallback mapping construction produces misaligned or empty mappings.
   141→
   142→---
   143→
   144→## Data Flow Diagram Showing Where Breaks Occur
   145→
   146→```
   147→Protocol PDF
   148→    |
   149→    v
   150→[Extract Node] --> Gemini extracts text, thresholds, temporal constraints
   151→    |                 (numeric_thresholds have flat comparator/value/unit)
   152→    v
   153→[Ground Node]  --> TerminologyRouter + MedGemma + OMOP Mapper
   154→    |                 entity.selected_code = "C0001948" (UMLS)
   155→    |                 entity.omop_concept_id = "201826"
   156→    |                 entity.selected_system = "umls"
   157→    v
   158→[Field Mapper] --> Gemini decomposes to entity/relation/value/unit
   159→    |                 PRODUCES: { relation: "has", value: "6", unit: "months",
   160→    |                             entity_concept_id: "C0001948",       <-- KEY NAME
   161→    |                             entity_concept_system: "umls" }      <-- KEY NAME
   162→    v
   163→[Persist Node] --> Stores in criteria.conditions.field_mappings
   164→    |
   165→    v
   166→[API GET]      --> Returns criteria with conditions.field_mappings
   167→    |
   168→    v                         === FRONTEND BOUNDARY ===
   169→    |
   170→[buildInitialValues] --> Priority 1: reads field_mappings
   171→    |   BREAK 1: value is flat string "6", not { type: "standard", value: "6", unit: "months" }
   172→    |   BREAK 2: relation is "has"/"is", not in RelationOperator enum
   173→    |   BREAK 3: looks for entity_code/entity_system, finds entity_concept_id/entity_concept_system
   174→    v
   175→[StructuredFieldEditor] --> Renders with empty values/relations
   176→    |   BREAK 4: no omop_concept_id field in FieldMapping type
   177→    v
   178→[UI Display] --> Raw text entities, "Select relation...", empty Value/Unit fields
   179→```
   180→
   181→---
   182→
   183→## Database State Analysis
   184→
   185→### Schema Correctness
   186→The database schema is well-designed:
   187→- `atomic_criteria` table has all the right columns: `entity_concept_id`, `entity_concept_system`, `omop_concept_id`, `relation_operator`, `value_numeric`, `value_text`, `unit_text`, `unit_concept_id`, `value_concept_id`
   188→- `criteria.conditions` JSONB stores field_mappings correctly
   189→- `entity` table stores grounding results with all terminology codes
   190→
   191→### The Problem Is Not the DB
   192→The data in the DB is likely correct (grounding codes are persisted in Entity records and AtomicCriterion records). The problem is:
   193→1. The `field_mappings` dicts stored in `criteria.conditions` use **different key names** than the frontend expects
   194→2. The `field_mappings` value format is **flat** (string) not **typed** (object with type discriminator)
   195→3. AtomicCriterion data (which has OMOP IDs) is **never included in the API response** for the review endpoint
   196→
   197→### Verification Query
   198→To confirm, run against the app DB:
   199→```sql
   200→-- Check what field_mappings look like in the DB
   201→SELECT id, conditions->'field_mappings'->0 AS first_mapping
   202→FROM criteria
   203→WHERE conditions IS NOT NULL
   204→  AND conditions->>'field_mappings' IS NOT NULL
   205→LIMIT 5;
   206→
   207→-- Check if entities have grounding codes
   208→SELECT id, text, umls_cui, snomed_code, grounding_system, grounding_confidence
   209→FROM entity
   210→WHERE grounding_confidence > 0
   211→LIMIT 10;
   212→
   213→-- Check atomic_criteria for OMOP data
   214→SELECT id, entity_concept_id, entity_concept_system, omop_concept_id,
   215→       relation_operator, value_numeric, value_text, unit_text
   216→FROM atomic_criteria
   217→LIMIT 10;
   218→```
   219→
   220→---
   221→
   222→## MLFlow Tracing Analysis
   223→
   224→### What's Tracked
   225→MLFlow traces the 7-node pipeline via `pipeline_span()`:
   226→- `ground_node`: Logs `grounded_count`, `error_count`, `avg_entity_ms`, `retry_count`
   227→- `persist_node`: Logs `entities_persisted`, `status`, `error_count`
   228→- `structure_node`: Logs `criteria_processed`, `errors`
   229→- Circuit breaker state changes (Gemini, UMLS, ToolUniverse, etc.)
   230→
   231→### What's Missing from MLFlow
   232→- **No per-entity grounding confidence logged as a metric** — confidence is only in AuditLog DB table
   233→- **No field_mapping generation success/failure metrics** — field_mapper failures are silently caught and return `[]`
   234→- **No reconciliation status distribution** — how many entities are "agree" vs "omop_missing" vs "disagreement"
   235→- **No schema validation checks** — whether produced field_mappings match frontend expectations
   236→
   237→### How to Debug via MLFlow
   238→1. Open `http://localhost:5001`, select "protocol-processing" experiment
   239→2. Filter by protocol_id tag
   240→3. Look at `ground_node` span: if `error_count > 0` or `grounded_count < entity_count`, grounding failed for some entities
   241→4. Look at `persist_node` span: if `error_count > 0`, some entities failed to persist
   242→
   243→### How to Debug via AuditLog
   244→```sql
   245→-- Find grounding decisions with low confidence
   246→SELECT details->>'entity_text', details->>'confidence',
   247→       details->>'selected_code', details->>'selected_system',
   248→       details->>'reconciliation_status', details->>'reasoning'
   249→FROM audit_log
   250→WHERE event_type = 'entity_grounded'
   251→  AND (details->>'confidence')::float < 0.5
   252→ORDER BY created_at DESC
   253→LIMIT 20;
   254→```
   255→
   256→---

```
     1→"""Field mapping generation tool for grounded entities.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "Generate suggested field_mappings during grounding (ground node)"
     4→Per CONTEXT.md: "Criteria should be decomposed per AutoCriteria pattern: separate
     5→Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time"
     6→Per user decision: "MedGemma and Gemini collaborate: Gemini uses MedGemma as
     7→medical expert"
     8→
     9→Uses Gemini to generate suggested field mappings for the grounded entity +
    10→criterion text. These are best-effort suggestions — reviewer can edit in UI.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import logging
    16→from typing import Any
    17→
    18→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    21→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
    22→    create_structured_llm,
    23→    parse_structured_output,
    24→)
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→
    29→class FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):
    30→    """A single AutoCriteria field mapping decomposition.
    31→
    32→    Per the AutoCriteria pattern, each criterion is decomposed into
    33→    separate Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, and Time components.
    34→    """
    35→
    36→    entity: str = Field(description="The medical entity name (e.g. 'HbA1c')")
    37→    relation: str = Field(
    38→        description="The logical operator/relation (e.g. '<', '>', '=', 'has', 'is')"
    39→    )
    40→    value: str = Field(
    41→        description=(
    42→            "The specific value or threshold (e.g. '7%', 'positive', 'confirmed')"
    43→        )
    44→    )
    45→    unit: str | None = Field(
    46→        default=None,
    47→        description="Optional unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')",
    48→    )
    49→
    50→
    51→class FieldMappingResponse(BaseModel):
    52→    """Gemini structured output for field mappings."""
    53→
    54→    mappings: list[FieldMappingItem] = Field(
    55→        default_factory=list,
    56→        description="List of AutoCriteria field mapping decompositions",
    57→    )
    58→
    59→
    60→async def generate_field_mappings(
    61→    entity: EntityGroundingResult,
    62→    criterion_text: str,
    63→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    64→    """Generate suggested field mappings for a grounded entity.
    65→
    66→    Uses Gemini to decompose the criterion text into AutoCriteria field mappings:
    67→    Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time components for each discrete condition.
    68→
    69→    This is a best-effort suggestion — reviewer can edit in the UI. Errors
    70→    are logged and an empty list returned (not propagated as failures).
    71→
    72→    Args:
    73→        entity: Grounded EntityGroundingResult with code and preferred term.
    74→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
    75→
    76→    Returns:
    77→        List of field mapping dicts with keys: entity, relation, value, unit.
    78→        Empty list if generation fails.
    79→    """
    80→    if not criterion_text:
    81→        return []
    82→
    83→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)
    84→    if structured_llm is None:
    85→        return []
    86→
    87→    try:
    88→        # Build a context-rich prompt for field mapping generation
    89→        grounded_term = entity.preferred_term or entity.entity_text
    90→        code_context = ""
    91→        if entity.selected_code and entity.selected_system:
    92→            system = entity.selected_system.upper()
    93→            code_context = f"(grounded to {system} code: {entity.selected_code})"
    94→
    95→        prompt = (
    96→            "You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. Decompose the"
    97→            " following criterion into structured AutoCriteria field"
    98→            " mappings using the Entity-Relation-Value-Unit pattern.\n\n"
    99→            f"Medical entity: {grounded_term} {code_context}\n"
   100→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n\n"
   101→            "Instructions:\n"
   102→            "- Extract each discrete measurement, threshold, or condition"
   103→            " as a separate mapping\n"
   104→            "- entity: the specific measurement or concept"
   105→            " (e.g. 'HbA1c', 'Age', 'eGFR')\n"
   106→            "- relation: the logical operator"
   107→            " (<, >, >=, <=, =, has, is, within, not)\n"
   108→            "- value: the specific threshold or value"
   109→            " (e.g. '7', 'positive', 'confirmed')\n"
   110→            "- unit: the unit if applicable (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')"
   111→            " or null\n"
   112→            "- Create one mapping per discrete condition in the criterion\n"
   113→            "- If no clear measurement exists, create one mapping with"
   114→            " relation='has', value='confirmed'"
   115→        )
   116→
   117→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   118→        response = parse_structured_output(result, FieldMappingResponse)
   119→
   120→        mappings = [
   121→            {
   122→                "entity": m.entity,
   123→                "relation": m.relation,
   124→                "value": m.value,
   125→                "unit": m.unit,
   126→                "entity_concept_id": entity.selected_code,
   127→                "entity_concept_system": entity.selected_system,
   128→                "omop_concept_id": entity.omop_concept_id,
   129→                "entity_type": entity.entity_type,
   130→            }
   131→            for m in response.mappings
   132→        ]
   133→
   134→        logger.info(
   135→            "Generated %d field mapping(s) for entity '%s'",
   136→            len(mappings),
   137→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   138→        )
   139→        return mappings
   140→
   141→    except Exception as e:
   142→        logger.warning(
   143→            "Field mapping generation failed for entity '%s': %s",
   144→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   145→            e,
   146→            exc_info=True,
   147→        )
   148→        # Best-effort: return empty list on failure
   149→        return []
   150→
```

```
    30→export function ConfidenceBadge({ confidence }: { confidence: number }) {

    51→                colorClass
    52→            )}
    53→        >

    57→}

    97→
    98→export function formatTemporalConstraint(tc: Record<string, unknown>): string {
    99→    const duration = 'duration' in tc ? (tc.duration as string) : null;
   100→    const relation = 'relation' in tc ? (tc.relation as string) : null;
   101→    const referencePoint = 'reference_point' in tc ? (tc.reference_point as string) : null;
   102→
   103→    if (!duration) return '';
   104→
   105→    const relationMap: Record<string, string> = {
   106→        within: 'Within',
   107→        before: 'Before',
   108→        after: 'After',
   109→        at_least: 'At least',
   110→    };
   111→
   112→    const relationText = relation ? relationMap[relation] || relation : '';
   113→    const parts = [relationText, duration];
   114→    if (referencePoint) parts.push('of', referencePoint);
   115→
   116→    return parts.filter(Boolean).join(' ');
   117→}
   118→
   119→export function formatNumericThreshold(threshold: Record<string, unknown>): string {
   120→    const value = 'value' in threshold ? threshold.value : null;
   121→    const unit = 'unit' in threshold ? (threshold.unit as string) : '';
   122→    const comparator = 'comparator' in threshold ? (threshold.comparator as string) : '';
   123→    const upperValue = 'upper_value' in threshold ? threshold.upper_value : null;
   124→
   125→    if (value === null) return '';
   126→
   127→    if (comparator === 'range' && upperValue !== null) {
   128→        return `${value}-${upperValue} ${unit}`.trim();
   129→    }
   130→
   131→    return `${comparator}${value} ${unit}`.trim();
   132→}
   133→
   134→export function extractThresholdsList(
   135→    nt: Record<string, unknown> | null
   136→): Array<Record<string, unknown>> {
   137→    if (!nt) return [];
   138→
   139→    // Shape 1: {"thresholds": [...]} wrapper object
   140→    if ('thresholds' in nt && Array.isArray(nt.thresholds)) {
   141→        return nt.thresholds as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
   142→    }
   143→
   144→    // Shape 2: raw array stored directly
   145→    if (Array.isArray(nt)) {
   146→        return nt as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
   147→    }
   148→
   149→    // Shape 3: single threshold object without wrapper
   150→    if ('value' in nt && 'comparator' in nt) {
   151→        return [nt];
   152→    }
   153→
   154→    return [];
   155→}
   156→
   157→/** Map extraction comparator strings to RelationOperator. */
   158→function mapComparator(comparator: string): RelationOperator | '' {
   159→    const map: Record<string, RelationOperator> = {
   160→        '>=': '>=',
   161→        '<=': '<=',
   162→        '>': '>',
   163→        '<': '<',
   164→        '==': '=',
   165→        '=': '=',
   166→        range: 'within',
   167→    };
   168→    return map[comparator] ?? '';
   169→}
   170→
   171→/** Parse a duration string like "6 months" into {value, unit}. */
   172→function parseDuration(duration: string): {
   173→    value: string;
   174→    unit: TemporalUnit;
   175→} {
   176→    const match = duration.match(/^(\d+)\s*(days?|weeks?|months?|years?)$/i);
   177→    if (match) {
   178→        const raw = match[2].toLowerCase().replace(/s$/, '');
   179→        const unitMap: Record<string, TemporalUnit> = {
   180→            day: 'days',
   181→            week: 'weeks',
   182→            month: 'months',
   183→            year: 'years',
   184→        };
   185→        return { value: match[1], unit: unitMap[raw] ?? 'days' };
   186→    }
   187→    return { value: duration, unit: 'days' };
   188→}
   189→
   190→/**
   191→ * Build initial form values for the structured editor from a criterion's
   192→ * AI-extracted data (numeric_thresholds, temporal_constraint, conditions).
   193→ */
   194→export function buildInitialValues(criterion: Criterion): StructuredFieldFormValues {
   195→    // Priority 1: existing field_mappings from a previous structured edit
   196→    const cond = criterion.conditions as Record<string, unknown> | null;
   197→    if (cond && 'field_mappings' in cond && Array.isArray(cond.field_mappings)) {
   198→        const fms = cond.field_mappings as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
   199→        const mappings: FieldMapping[] = fms.map((fm) => {
   200→            const rel = (fm.relation as string) ?? '';
   201→            const rawVal = fm.value as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   202→            let value: FieldValue = { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' };
   203→            if (rawVal && rawVal.type === 'range') {
   204→                value = {
   205→                    type: 'range',
   206→                    min: String(rawVal.min ?? ''),
   207→                    max: String(rawVal.max ?? ''),
   208→                    unit: String(rawVal.unit ?? ''),
   209→                };
   210→            } else if (rawVal && rawVal.type === 'temporal') {
   211→                value = {
   212→                    type: 'temporal',
   213→                    duration: String(rawVal.duration ?? ''),
   214→                    unit: (rawVal.unit as TemporalUnit) ?? 'days',
   215→                };
   216→            } else if (rawVal && rawVal.type === 'standard') {
   217→                value = {
   218→                    type: 'standard',
   219→                    value: String(rawVal.value ?? ''),
   220→                    unit: String(rawVal.unit ?? ''),
   221→                };
   222→            }
   223→            return {
   224→                entity: String(fm.entity ?? ''),
   225→                entity_code: fm.entity_code ? String(fm.entity_code) : undefined,
   226→                entity_system: fm.entity_system ? String(fm.entity_system) : undefined,
   227→                relation: (rel as RelationOperator) || '',
   228→                value,
   229→            };
   230→        });
   231→        if (mappings.length > 0) return { mappings };
   232→    }
   233→
   234→    // Priority 2: infer from AI-extracted data (entities + thresholds + temporal)
   235→    const mappings: FieldMapping[] = [];
   236→
   237→    const entities = criterion.entities ?? [];
   238→    const thresholds = extractThresholdsList(criterion.numeric_thresholds);
   239→
   240→    const entityLabel = (e: { preferred_term: string | null; text: string }) =>
   241→        e.preferred_term || e.text;
   242→
   243→    const measurableEntities = entities.filter(
   244→        (e) =>
   245→            e.entity_type === 'Lab_Value' ||
   246→            e.entity_type === 'Biomarker' ||
   247→            e.entity_type === 'Demographic'
   248→    );
   249→
   250→    for (let i = 0; i < thresholds.length; i++) {
   251→        const t = thresholds[i];
   252→        const comparator = (t.comparator as string) ?? '';
   253→        const val = t.value as number | null;
   254→        const unit = (t.unit as string) ?? '';
   255→        const upperVal = t.upper_value as number | null;
   256→
   257→        if (val === null || val === undefined) continue;
   258→
   259→        const matchedEntity = measurableEntities[i];
   260→        const entity = matchedEntity ? entityLabel(matchedEntity) : '';
   261→        const entityCode = matchedEntity?.snomed_code ?? matchedEntity?.umls_cui ?? undefined;
   262→        const entitySystem = matchedEntity?.snomed_code
   263→            ? 'snomed'
   264→            : matchedEntity?.umls_cui
   265→              ? 'umls'
   266→              : undefined;
   267→
   268→        if (comparator === 'range' && upperVal != null) {
   269→            mappings.push({
   270→                entity,
   271→                entity_code: entityCode,
   272→                entity_system: entitySystem,
   273→                relation: 'within' as RelationOperator,
   274→                value: { type: 'range', min: String(val), max: String(upperVal), unit },
   275→            });
   276→        } else {
   277→            const relation = mapComparator(comparator);
   278→            mappings.push({
   279→                entity,
   280→                entity_code: entityCode,
   281→                entity_system: entitySystem,
   282→                relation,
   283→                value: { type: 'standard', value: String(val), unit },
   284→            });
   285→        }
   286→    }
   287→
   288→    const unmatchedEntities = entities.filter(
   289→        (e) =>
   290→            e.entity_type === 'Condition' ||
   291→            e.entity_type === 'Medication' ||
   292→            e.entity_type === 'Procedure'
   293→    );
   294→    for (const e of unmatchedEntities) {
   295→        const code = e.snomed_code ?? e.umls_cui ?? undefined;
   296→        const system = e.snomed_code ? 'snomed' : e.umls_cui ? 'umls' : undefined;
   297→        mappings.push({
   298→            entity: entityLabel(e),
   299→            entity_code: code,
   300→            entity_system: system,
   301→            relation: '',
   302→            value: { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' },
   303→        });
   304→    }
   305→
   306→    if (criterion.temporal_constraint) {
   307→        const tc = criterion.temporal_constraint;
   308→        const duration = tc.duration as string | undefined;
   309→        const referencePoint = tc.reference_point as string | undefined;
   310→
   311→        if (duration) {
   312→            const parsed = parseDuration(duration);
   313→            mappings.push({
   314→                entity: referencePoint ?? '',
   315→                relation: 'not_in_last' as RelationOperator,
   316→                value: { type: 'temporal', duration: parsed.value, unit: parsed.unit },
   317→            });
   318→        }
   319→    }
   320→
   321→    if (mappings.length === 0) return DEFAULT_FIELD_VALUES;
   322→    return { mappings };
   323→}
   324→
   325→export default function CriterionCard({
   326→    criterion,
   327→    onAction,
   328→    isSubmitting,
   329→    onCriterionClick,
   330→    isActive,
   331→    pdfUrl,
   332→}: CriterionCardProps) {
   333→    const [modifyDialogOpen, setModifyDialogOpen] = useState(false);
   334→    const [rejectDialogOpen, setRejectDialogOpen] = useState(false);
   335→
   336→    function handleApprove() {
   337→        onAction(criterion.id, {
   338→            action: 'approve',
   339→            reviewer_id: 'current-user',
   340→        });
   341→    }
   342→
   343→    function handleReject() {
   344→        setRejectDialogOpen(true);
   345→    }
   346→
   347→    function handleRejectConfirm(data: { reasons: string[]; comment?: string }) {
   348→        onAction(criterion.id, {
   349→            action: 'reject',
   350→            reviewer_id: 'current-user',
   351→            reject_reasons: data.reasons,
   352→            comment: data.comment,
   353→        });
   354→    }
   355→
   356→    return (
   357→        <div
   358→            className={cn(
   359→                'rounded-lg border bg-card p-4 shadow-sm border-l-4',
   360→                criterion.review_status === 'approved' && 'border-l-green-500',
   361→                criterion.review_status === 'rejected' && 'border-l-red-500',
   362→                criterion.review_status === 'modified' && 'border-l-blue-500',
   363→                !criterion.review_status && 'border-l-yellow-400'
   364→            )}
   365→        >
   366→            {/* Header row */}
   367→            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mb-3">
   368→                <CriteriaTypeBadge type={criterion.criteria_type} />
   369→                {criterion.category && (
   370→                    <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-gray-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">
   371→                        {criterion.category}
   372→                    </span>
   373→                )}
   374→                <ConfidenceBadge confidence={criterion.confidence} />
   375→                <CriterionReviewStatusBadge status={criterion.review_status} />
   376→                {criterion.page_number != null && (
   377→                    <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 ml-auto">
   378→                        p.{criterion.page_number}
   379→                    </span>
   380→                )}
   381→            </div>
   382→
   383→            {/* Body - criterion text (read-only, clickable for PDF navigation) */}
   384→            <p
   385→                className={cn(
   386→                    'text-sm text-foreground mb-3',
   387→                    criterion.page_number != null &&
   388→                        'cursor-pointer hover:bg-accent/50 rounded px-1 -mx-1 transition-colors',
   389→                    isActive && 'bg-accent/30 rounded px-1 -mx-1'
   390→                )}
   391→                onClick={() => {
   392→                    if (criterion.page_number != null && onCriterionClick) {
   393→                        onCriterionClick(criterion);
   394→                    }
   395→                }}
   396→                onKeyDown={(e) => {
   397→                    if (
   398→                        (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') &&
   399→                        criterion.page_number != null &&
   400→                        onCriterionClick
   401→                    ) {
   402→                        onCriterionClick(criterion);
   403→                    }
   404→                }}
   405→                role={criterion.page_number != null ? 'button' : undefined}
   406→                tabIndex={criterion.page_number != null ? 0 : undefined}
   407→                title={
   408→                    criterion.page_number != null
   409→                        ? `Click to view source (page ${criterion.page_number})`
   410→                        : undefined
   411→                }
   412→            >
   413→                {criterion.text}
   414→            </p>
   415→
   416→            {/* Field mapping badges */}
   417→            <FieldMappingBadges
   418→                criterion={criterion}
   419→                onEditClick={() => setModifyDialogOpen(true)}
   420→            />
   421→
   422→            {/* Assertion status tag */}
   423→            {criterion.assertion_status && criterion.assertion_status !== 'PRESENT' && (
   424→                <div className="mb-3">
   425→                    <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-purple-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-purple-800">
   426→                        {criterion.assertion_status}
   427→                    </span>
   428→                </div>
   429→            )}
   430→
   431→            {/* Temporal constraint */}
   432→            {criterion.temporal_constraint &&
   433→                formatTemporalConstraint(criterion.temporal_constraint) && (
   434→                    <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5">
   435→                        <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-indigo-600" />
   436→                        <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-indigo-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-indigo-800">
   437→                            {formatTemporalConstraint(criterion.temporal_constraint)}
   438→                        </span>
   439→                    </div>
   440→                )}
   441→
   442→            {/* Numeric thresholds */}
   443→            {extractThresholdsList(criterion.numeric_thresholds).length > 0 && (
   444→                <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
   445→                    <Hash className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-teal-600" />
   446→                    {extractThresholdsList(criterion.numeric_thresholds).map((threshold) => {
   447→                        const text = formatNumericThreshold(threshold);
   448→                        return text ? (
   449→                            <span
   450→                                key={text}
   451→                                className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-teal-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-teal-800"
   452→                            >
   453→                                {text}
   454→                            </span>
   455→                        ) : null;
   456→                    })}
   457→                </div>
   458→            )}
   459→
   460→            {/* Action buttons */}
   461→            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-2 border-t">
   462→                <Button
   463→                    size="sm"
   464→                    variant="outline"
   465→                    className="text-green-700 border-green-300 hover:bg-green-50"
   466→                    onClick={handleApprove}
   467→                    disabled={isSubmitting || criterion.review_status === 'approved'}
   468→                >
   469→                    {isSubmitting ? (
   470→                        <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin mr-1" />
   471→                    ) : (
   472→                        <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
   473→                    )}
   474→                    Approve
   475→                </Button>
   476→                <Button
   477→                    size="sm"
   478→                    variant="outline"
   479→                    className="text-red-700 border-red-300 hover:bg-red-50"
   480→                    onClick={handleReject}
   481→                    disabled={isSubmitting || criterion.review_status === 'rejected'}
   482→                >
   483→                    {isSubmitting ? (
   484→                        <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin mr-1" />
   485→                    ) : (
   486→                        <XCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
   487→                    )}
   488→                    Reject
   489→                </Button>
   490→                <Button
   491→                    size="sm"
   492→                    variant="outline"
   493→                    className="text-blue-700 border-blue-300 hover:bg-blue-50"
   494→                    onClick={() => setModifyDialogOpen(true)}
   495→                    disabled={isSubmitting}
   496→                >
   497→                    <Pencil className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
   498→                    Modify
   499→                </Button>
   500→                <CriterionRerunPanel
   501→                    criterionId={criterion.id}
   502→                    criterionText={criterion.text}
   503→                    currentExtraction={{
   504→                        criteria_type: criterion.criteria_type,
   505→                        category: criterion.category,
   506→                        temporal_constraint: criterion.temporal_constraint,
   507→                        conditions: criterion.conditions,
   508→                        numeric_thresholds: criterion.numeric_thresholds,
   509→                        text: criterion.text,
   510→                    }}
   511→                    onAccept={(revised) => {
   512→                        onAction(criterion.id, {
   513→                            action: 'modify',
   514→                            reviewer_id: 'current-user',
   515→                            modified_text: revised.text as string | undefined,
   516→                            modified_type: revised.criteria_type as string | undefined,
   517→                            modified_category: revised.category as string | undefined,
   518→                            modified_structured_fields: revised,
   519→                            comment: 'AI-assisted correction with reviewer feedback',
   520→                        });
   521→                    }}
   522→                    trigger={
   523→                        <Button
   524→                            size="sm"
   525→                            variant="outline"
   526→                            className="text-purple-700 border-purple-300 hover:bg-purple-50"
   527→                            disabled={isSubmitting}
   528→                        >
   529→                            <Wand2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
   530→                            Correct with AI
   531→                        </Button>
   532→                    }
   533→                />
   534→            </div>
   535→
   536→            {/* Audit history section */}
   537→            <CriterionAuditHistory criterionId={criterion.id} />
   538→
   539→            <RejectDialog
   540→                open={rejectDialogOpen}
   541→                onOpenChange={setRejectDialogOpen}
   542→                onConfirm={handleRejectConfirm}
   543→            />
   544→
   545→            <CriterionModifyDialog
   546→                open={modifyDialogOpen}
   547→                onOpenChange={setModifyDialogOpen}
   548→                criterion={criterion}
   549→                onAction={(action) => onAction(criterion.id, action)}
   550→                isSubmitting={isSubmitting}
   551→                pdfUrl={pdfUrl}
   552→            />
   553→        </div>
   554→    );
   555→}
   556→
```

```
     1→import type { Criterion } from '../hooks/useReviews';
     2→
     3→interface FieldMappingBadgesProps {
     4→    criterion: Criterion;
     5→    onEditClick?: () => void;
     6→}
     7→
     8→function formatMappingValue(value: Record<string, unknown>): string {
     9→    if (typeof value !== 'object') return '';
    10→    if (value.type === 'range')
    11→        return `${value.min}–${value.max}${value.unit ? ` ${value.unit}` : ''}`;
    12→    if (value.type === 'temporal') return `${value.duration} ${value.unit}`;
    13→    if (value.type === 'standard') return `${value.value}${value.unit ? ` ${value.unit}` : ''}`;
    14→    return '';
    15→}
    16→
    17→function renderValue(value: unknown): string {
    18→    if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
    19→    if (typeof value === 'string') return value;
    20→    if (typeof value === 'number') return String(value);
    21→    if (typeof value === 'object') return formatMappingValue(value as Record<string, unknown>);
    22→    return '';
    23→}
    24→
    25→export default function FieldMappingBadges({ criterion, onEditClick }: FieldMappingBadgesProps) {
    26→    const cond = criterion.conditions as Record<string, unknown> | null;
    27→    const fieldMappings =
    28→        cond && 'field_mappings' in cond && Array.isArray(cond.field_mappings)
    29→            ? (cond.field_mappings as Array<{
    30→                  entity: string;
    31→                  entity_code?: string;
    32→                  entity_system?: string;
    33→                  relation: string;
    34→                  value: unknown;
    35→              }>)
    36→            : null;
    37→
    38→    if (!fieldMappings || fieldMappings.length === 0) return null;
    39→
    40→    const badgeContent = (mapping: (typeof fieldMappings)[number]) => {
    41→        const valueText = renderValue(mapping.value);
    42→        return (
    43→            <>
    44→                <span className="font-semibold text-blue-900">{mapping.entity || '—'}</span>
    45→                {mapping.entity_code && (
    46→                    <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-green-50 border border-green-200 px-1.5 py-0 text-[10px] text-green-700">
    47→                        {mapping.entity_system?.toUpperCase()}: {mapping.entity_code}
    48→                    </span>
    49→                )}
    50→                {mapping.relation && (
    51→                    <span className="text-blue-600 font-mono text-xs">{mapping.relation}</span>
    52→                )}
    53→                {mapping.relation && valueText ? (
    54→                    <span className="text-blue-800">{valueText}</span>
    55→                ) : mapping.relation && !valueText ? (
    56→                    <span className="text-muted-foreground/50 italic text-xs">no value</span>
    57→                ) : null}
    58→            </>
    59→        );
    60→    };
    61→
    62→    return (
    63→        <div className="mb-3">
    64→            <div className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground mb-2">Field Mappings</div>
    65→            <div className="space-y-1">
    66→                {fieldMappings.map((mapping, idx) => (
    67→                    // biome-ignore lint/suspicious/noArrayIndexKey: field_mappings have no stable unique id
    68→                    <div key={idx}>
    69→                        {idx > 0 && (
    70→                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 py-1">
    71→                                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-purple-600 bg-purple-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
    72→                                    AND
    73→                                </span>
    74→                                <div className="flex-1 border-t border-dashed border-muted" />
    75→                            </div>
    76→                        )}
    77→                        {onEditClick ? (
    78→                            <button
    79→                                onClick={onEditClick}
    80→                                className="w-full text-left flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border bg-blue-50/50 border-blue-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-blue-100/50 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
    81→                                title="Click to edit field mappings"
    82→                                type="button"
    83→                            >
    84→                                {badgeContent(mapping)}
    85→                            </button>
    86→                        ) : (
    87→                            <div className="w-full flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border bg-blue-50/50 border-blue-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm">
    88→                                {badgeContent(mapping)}
    89→                            </div>
    90→                        )}
    91→                    </div>
    92→                ))}
    93→            </div>
    94→        </div>
    95→    );
    96→}
    97→
```

```
     1→// TypeScript types for structured editor form
     2→
     3→export type RelationOperator =
     4→    | '='
     5→    | '!='
     6→    | '>'
     7→    | '>='
     8→    | '<'
     9→    | '<='
    10→    | 'within'
    11→    | 'not_in_last'
    12→    | 'contains'
    13→    | 'not_contains';
    14→
    15→export type RelationCategory = 'standard' | 'range' | 'temporal';
    16→
    17→export type TemporalUnit = 'days' | 'weeks' | 'months' | 'years';
    18→
    19→export interface StandardValue {
    20→    type: 'standard';
    21→    value: string;
    22→    unit: string;
    23→}
    24→
    25→export interface RangeValue {
    26→    type: 'range';
    27→    min: string;
    28→    max: string;
    29→    unit: string;
    30→}
    31→
    32→export interface TemporalValue {
    33→    type: 'temporal';
    34→    duration: string;
    35→    unit: TemporalUnit;
    36→}
    37→
    38→export type FieldValue = StandardValue | RangeValue | TemporalValue;
    39→
    40→// Single field mapping with all possible fields
    41→export interface FieldMapping {
    42→    entity: string;
    43→    entity_code?: string;
    44→    entity_system?: string;
    45→    relation: RelationOperator | '';
    46→    value: FieldValue;
    47→}
    48→
    49→// Form values now contain an array of mappings
    50→export interface StructuredFieldFormValues {
    51→    mappings: FieldMapping[];
    52→}
    53→
    54→export interface StructuredFieldEditorProps {
    55→    criterionId: string;
    56→    initialValues?: Partial<StructuredFieldFormValues>;
    57→    onSave: (values: StructuredFieldFormValues) => void;
    58→    onCancel: () => void;
    59→    isSubmitting?: boolean;
    60→}
    61→
```

```
     1→// Main structured field editor component with multiple entity/relation/value mappings
     2→
     3→import { Loader2, Plus, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
     4→import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
     5→import { Controller, useFieldArray, useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
     6→
     7→import type { TerminologySearchResult } from '../../hooks/useTerminologySearch';
     8→import type { TerminologySystem } from '../TerminologyBadge';
     9→import { TerminologyCombobox } from '../TerminologyCombobox';
    10→import { Button } from '../ui/Button';
    11→import { RelationSelect } from './RelationSelect';
    12→import { ValueInput } from './ValueInput';
    13→import {
    14→    DEFAULT_FIELD_VALUES,
    15→    DEFAULT_MAPPING,
    16→    RELATION_CATEGORY_MAP,
    17→    getDefaultValueForCategory,
    18→} from './constants';
    19→import type {
    20→    RelationOperator,
    21→    StructuredFieldEditorProps,
    22→    StructuredFieldFormValues,
    23→} from './types';
    24→
    25→const TERMINOLOGY_SYSTEMS: Array<{ value: TerminologySystem; label: string }> = [
    26→    { value: 'snomed', label: 'SNOMED' },
    27→    { value: 'loinc', label: 'LOINC' },
    28→    { value: 'rxnorm', label: 'RxNorm' },
    29→    { value: 'icd10', label: 'ICD-10' },
    30→    { value: 'hpo', label: 'HPO' },
    31→    { value: 'umls', label: 'UMLS' },
    32→];
    33→
    34→export function StructuredFieldEditor({
    35→    criterionId,
    36→    initialValues,
    37→    onSave,
    38→    onCancel,
    39→    isSubmitting = false,
    40→}: StructuredFieldEditorProps) {
    41→    const { control, handleSubmit, watch, setValue } = useForm<StructuredFieldFormValues>({
    42→        defaultValues: initialValues ?? DEFAULT_FIELD_VALUES,
    43→    });
    44→
    45→    const { fields, append, remove } = useFieldArray({
    46→        control,
    47→        name: 'mappings',
    48→    });
    49→
    50→    // Per-mapping terminology system selector state
    51→    const [systemSelections, setSystemSelections] = useState<Record<number, TerminologySystem>>(
    52→        () => {
    53→            const initial: Record<number, TerminologySystem> = {};
    54→            const mappings = initialValues?.mappings ?? DEFAULT_FIELD_VALUES.mappings;
    55→            for (let i = 0; i < mappings.length; i++) {
    56→                initial[i] = (mappings[i].entity_system as TerminologySystem) || 'snomed';
    57→            }
    58→            return initial;
    59→        }
    60→    );
    61→
    62→    const getSystem = (index: number): TerminologySystem => systemSelections[index] ?? 'snomed';
    63→
    64→    const setSystem = (index: number, system: TerminologySystem) =>
    65→        setSystemSelections((prev) => ({ ...prev, [index]: system }));
    66→
    67→    // Watch all mappings for validation
    68→    const allMappings = watch('mappings');
    69→
    70→    // Track previous relations for each mapping to detect changes
    71→    const previousRelationsRef = useRef<Array<RelationOperator | ''>>([]);
    72→
    73→    // State cleanup on relation change - prevent state leak between categories
    74→    useEffect(() => {
    75→        allMappings.forEach((mapping, index) => {
    76→            const currentRelation = mapping.relation;
    77→            const previousRelation = previousRelationsRef.current[index] ?? '';
    78→
    79→            // Skip on initial mount (previousRelation is empty string initially)
    80→            if (previousRelation === '' && currentRelation === '') {
    81→                return;
    82→            }
    83→
    84→            // If relation actually changed
    85→            if (previousRelation !== currentRelation && currentRelation !== '') {
    86→                const newCategory = RELATION_CATEGORY_MAP[currentRelation as RelationOperator];
    87→                const currentCategory = mapping.value.type;
    88→
    89→                // If category changed, reset value to default for new category
    90→                if (newCategory !== currentCategory) {
    91→                    setValue(`mappings.${index}.value`, getDefaultValueForCategory(newCategory));
    92→                }
    93→            }
    94→
    95→            // Update ref for next change detection
    96→            previousRelationsRef.current[index] = currentRelation;
    97→        });
    98→    }, [allMappings, setValue]);
    99→
   100→    // Check if we can add a new mapping (last mapping must have entity filled)
   101→    const canAddMapping = () => {
   102→        if (fields.length === 0) return true;
   103→        const lastMapping = allMappings[allMappings.length - 1];
   104→        return lastMapping && lastMapping.entity.trim() !== '';
   105→    };
   106→
   107→    const handleAddMapping = () => {
   108→        if (canAddMapping()) {
   109→            append(DEFAULT_MAPPING);
   110→        }
   111→    };
   112→
   113→    return (
   114→        <div className="space-y-4">
   115→            {/* Mapping cards */}
   116→            {fields.map((field, index) => {
   117→                const mapping = allMappings[index];
   118→                const relationCategory =
   119→                    mapping?.relation !== ''
   120→                        ? RELATION_CATEGORY_MAP[mapping.relation as RelationOperator]
   121→                        : null;
   122→
   123→                return (
   124→                    <div key={field.id} className="border rounded-lg p-3 mb-3 relative">
   125→                        {/* Mapping label and remove button */}
   126→                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
   127→                            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">
   128→                                Mapping {index + 1}
   129→                            </span>
   130→                            <Button
   131→                                type="button"
   132→                                size="sm"
   133→                                variant="ghost"
   134→                                onClick={() => remove(index)}
   135→                                disabled={fields.length === 1 || isSubmitting}
   136→                                className="h-6 w-6 p-0"
   137→                            >
   138→                                <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4 text-red-600" />
   139→                            </Button>
   140→                        </div>
   141→
   142→                        {/* Entity field with terminology search */}
   143→                        <div className="mb-3">
   144→                            <label
   145→                                htmlFor={`entity-${criterionId}-${index}`}
   146→                                className="block text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground mb-1"
   147→                            >
   148→                                Entity
   149→                            </label>
   150→                            <div className="flex gap-2">
   151→                                <select
   152→                                    value={getSystem(index)}
   153→                                    onChange={(e) =>
   154→                                        setSystem(index, e.target.value as TerminologySystem)
   155→                                    }
   156→                                    disabled={isSubmitting}
   157→                                    className="rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-2 py-2 text-sm ring-offset-background focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed w-[100px] shrink-0"
   158→                                >
   159→                                    {TERMINOLOGY_SYSTEMS.map((s) => (
   160→                                        <option key={s.value} value={s.value}>
   161→                                            {s.label}
   162→                                        </option>
   163→                                    ))}
   164→                                </select>
   165→                                <Controller
   166→                                    name={`mappings.${index}.entity`}
   167→                                    control={control}
   168→                                    render={({ field }) => (
   169→                                        <div className="flex-1">
   170→                                            <TerminologyCombobox
   171→                                                system={getSystem(index)}
   172→                                                value={field.value}
   173→                                                onChange={(val) => {
   174→                                                    field.onChange(val);
   175→                                                    // Clear code/system when user types freely
   176→                                                    setValue(
   177→                                                        `mappings.${index}.entity_code`,
   178→                                                        undefined
   179→                                                    );
   180→                                                    setValue(
   181→                                                        `mappings.${index}.entity_system`,
   182→                                                        undefined
   183→                                                    );
   184→                                                }}
   185→                                                onSelect={(result: TerminologySearchResult) => {
   186→                                                    field.onChange(result.display);
   187→                                                    setValue(
   188→                                                        `mappings.${index}.entity_code`,
   189→                                                        result.code
   190→                                                    );
   191→                                                    setValue(
   192→                                                        `mappings.${index}.entity_system`,
   193→                                                        getSystem(index)
   194→                                                    );
   195→                                                }}
   196→                                                placeholder="Search terminology..."
   197→                                            />
   198→                                        </div>
   199→                                    )}
   200→                                />
   201→                            </div>
   202→                            {mapping?.entity_code && (
   203→                                <div className="mt-1">
   204→                                    <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-green-50 border border-green-200 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-green-700">
   205→                                        {mapping.entity_system?.toUpperCase()}:{' '}
   206→                                        {mapping.entity_code}
   207→                                    </span>
   208→                                </div>
   209→                            )}
   210→                        </div>
   211→
   212→                        {/* Relation field */}
   213→                        <div className="mb-3">
   214→                            <label
   215→                                htmlFor={`relation-${criterionId}-${index}`}
   216→                                className="block text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground mb-1"
   217→                            >
   218→                                Relation
   219→                            </label>
   220→                            <Controller
   221→                                name={`mappings.${index}.relation`}
   222→                                control={control}
   223→                                render={({ field }) => (
   224→                                    <RelationSelect
   225→                                        value={field.value}
   226→                                        onChange={field.onChange}
   227→                                        disabled={isSubmitting}
   228→                                    />
   229→                                )}
   230→                            />
   231→                        </div>
   232→
   233→                        {/* Value field - conditionally rendered based on relation category */}
   234→                        {relationCategory && (
   235→                            <div>
   236→                                <Controller
   237→                                    name={`mappings.${index}.value`}
   238→                                    control={control}
   239→                                    render={({ field }) => (
   240→                                        <ValueInput
   241→                                            category={relationCategory}
   242→                                            value={field.value}
   243→                                            onChange={field.onChange}
   244→                                            disabled={isSubmitting}
   245→                                        />
   246→                                    )}
   247→                                />
   248→                            </div>
   249→                        )}
   250→                    </div>
   251→                );
   252→            })}
   253→
   254→            {/* Add Mapping button */}
   255→            <div className="mb-3">
   256→                <Button
   257→                    type="button"
   258→                    size="sm"
   259→                    variant="outline"
   260→                    onClick={handleAddMapping}
   261→                    disabled={!canAddMapping() || isSubmitting}
   262→                    className="w-full"
   263→                >
   264→                    <Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
   265→                    Add Mapping
   266→                </Button>
   267→                {!canAddMapping() && fields.length > 0 && (
   268→                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">
   269→                        Complete the current mapping before adding another
   270→                    </p>
   271→                )}
   272→            </div>
   273→
   274→            {/* Action buttons */}
   275→            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-2">
   276→                <Button size="sm" onClick={handleSubmit(onSave)} disabled={isSubmitting}>
   277→                    {isSubmitting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin mr-1" /> : null}
   278→                    Save
   279→                </Button>
   280→                <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={onCancel} disabled={isSubmitting}>
   281→                    Cancel
   282→                </Button>
   283→            </div>
   284→        </div>
   285→    );
   286→}
   287→
   288→// Export as both default and named export for flexible importing
   289→export default StructuredFieldEditor;
   290→
```

```
   126→
   127→// --- Hooks ---
   128→
   129→export function useBatchList(page: number, pageSize: number, status?: string) {
   130→    const params = new URLSearchParams({
   131→        page: String(page),
   132→        page_size: String(pageSize),
   133→    });
   134→    if (status) {
   135→        params.set('status', status);
   136→    }
   137→
   138→    return useQuery({
   139→        queryKey: ['review-batches', page, pageSize, status],
   140→        queryFn: () => fetchApi<BatchListResponse>(`/reviews/batches?${params.toString()}`),
   141→    });
   142→}
   143→
   144→export function useBatchesByProtocol(protocolId: string) {
   145→    return useQuery({
   146→        queryKey: ['review-batches', 'protocol', protocolId],
   147→        queryFn: () =>
   148→            fetchApi<BatchListResponse>(
   149→                `/reviews/batches?protocol_id=${encodeURIComponent(protocolId)}`
   150→            ),
   151→        enabled: !!protocolId,
   152→    });
   153→}
   154→
   155→export function useBatchCriteria(batchId: string, sortBy?: string, sortOrder?: string) {
   156→    const params = new URLSearchParams();
   157→    if (sortBy) {
   158→        params.set('sort_by', sortBy);
   159→    }
   160→    if (sortOrder) {
   161→        params.set('sort_order', sortOrder);
   162→    }
   163→    const queryString = params.toString();
   164→    const url = queryString
   165→        ? `/reviews/batches/${batchId}/criteria?${queryString}`
   166→        : `/reviews/batches/${batchId}/criteria`;
   167→
   168→    return useQuery({
   169→        queryKey: ['batch-criteria', batchId, sortBy, sortOrder],
   170→        queryFn: () => fetchApi<Criterion[]>(url),
   171→        enabled: Boolean(batchId),
   172→    });
   173→}
   174→
   175→export function useReviewAction() {
   176→    const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   177→
   178→    return useMutation({
   179→        mutationFn: ({ criteriaId, ...body }: ReviewActionRequest & { criteriaId: string }) =>
   180→            fetchApi<Criterion>(`/reviews/criteria/${criteriaId}/action`, {
   181→                method: 'POST',
   182→                body: JSON.stringify(body),
   183→            }),
   184→        onSuccess: () => {
   185→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['batch-criteria'] });
   186→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['review-batches'] });
   187→        },
   188→    });
   189→}
   190→
   191→export function usePdfUrl(protocolId: string) {
   192→    return useQuery({
   193→        queryKey: ['pdf-url', protocolId],
   194→        queryFn: () => fetchApi<PdfUrlResponse>(`/reviews/protocols/${protocolId}/pdf-url`),
   195→        enabled: Boolean(protocolId),
   196→        staleTime: 50 * 60 * 1000,
   197→    });
   198→}
   199→
   200→export function useAuditLog(
   201→    page: number,
   202→    pageSize: number,
   203→    targetType?: string,
   204→    targetId?: string,
   205→    batchId?: string
   206→) {
   207→    const params = new URLSearchParams({
   208→        page: String(page),
   209→        page_size: String(pageSize),
   210→    });
   211→    if (targetType) {
   212→        params.set('target_type', targetType);
   213→    }
   214→    if (targetId) {
   215→        params.set('target_id', targetId);
   216→    }
   217→    if (batchId) {
   218→        params.set('batch_id', batchId);
   219→    }
   220→
   221→    return useQuery({
   222→        queryKey: ['audit-log', page, pageSize, targetType, targetId, batchId],
   223→        queryFn: () => fetchApi<AuditLogListResponse>(`/reviews/audit-log?${params.toString()}`),
   224→        staleTime: 30_000,
   225→    });
   226→}
   227→
   228→export function usePendingSummary() {
   229→    return useQuery({
   230→        queryKey: ['pending-summary'],
   231→        queryFn: () => fetchApi<PendingSummary>('/reviews/pending-summary'),
   232→        staleTime: 30 * 1000,
   233→    });
   234→}
   235→
   236→export function usePipelineSummary() {
   237→    return useQuery({
   238→        queryKey: ['pipeline-summary'],
   239→        queryFn: () => fetchApi<PipelineSummary>('/reviews/pipeline-summary'),
   240→        staleTime: 30 * 1000,
   241→    });
   242→}
   243→
```

```
     1→// Relation configuration and constants
     2→
     3→import type {
     4→    FieldMapping,
     5→    FieldValue,
     6→    RelationCategory,
     7→    RelationOperator,
     8→    StructuredFieldFormValues,
     9→    TemporalUnit,
    10→} from './types';
    11→
    12→export const RELATIONS: Array<{
    13→    operator: RelationOperator;
    14→    label: string;
    15→    category: RelationCategory;
    16→}> = [
    17→    { operator: '=', label: 'equals (=)', category: 'standard' },
    18→    { operator: '!=', label: 'not equals (!=)', category: 'standard' },
    19→    { operator: '>', label: 'greater than (>)', category: 'standard' },
    20→    { operator: '>=', label: 'greater or equal (>=)', category: 'standard' },
    21→    { operator: '<', label: 'less than (<)', category: 'standard' },
    22→    { operator: '<=', label: 'less or equal (<=)', category: 'standard' },
    23→    { operator: 'within', label: 'within (range)', category: 'range' },
    24→    { operator: 'not_in_last', label: 'not in last (temporal)', category: 'temporal' },
    25→    { operator: 'contains', label: 'contains', category: 'standard' },
    26→    { operator: 'not_contains', label: 'does not contain', category: 'standard' },
    27→];
    28→
    29→export const RELATION_CATEGORY_MAP: Record<RelationOperator, RelationCategory> = RELATIONS.reduce(
    30→    (acc, rel) => {
    31→        acc[rel.operator] = rel.category;
    32→        return acc;
    33→    },
    34→    {} as Record<RelationOperator, RelationCategory>
    35→);
    36→
    37→export const TEMPORAL_UNITS: Array<{ value: TemporalUnit; label: string }> = [
    38→    { value: 'days', label: 'Days' },
    39→    { value: 'weeks', label: 'Weeks' },
    40→    { value: 'months', label: 'Months' },
    41→    { value: 'years', label: 'Years' },
    42→];
    43→
    44→export const DEFAULT_MAPPING: FieldMapping = {
    45→    entity: '',
    46→    relation: '' as const,
    47→    value: { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' },
    48→};
    49→
    50→export const DEFAULT_FIELD_VALUES: StructuredFieldFormValues = {
    51→    mappings: [DEFAULT_MAPPING],
    52→};
    53→
    54→export function getDefaultValueForCategory(category: RelationCategory): FieldValue {
    55→    switch (category) {
    56→        case 'standard':
    57→            return { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' };
    58→        case 'range':
    59→            return { type: 'range', min: '', max: '', unit: '' };
    60→        case 'temporal':
    61→            return { type: 'temporal', duration: '', unit: 'days' };
    62→    }
    63→}
    64→
```

```
     1→"""Add indexes to optimize export join patterns.
     2→
     3→Phase 4: Indexes for CIRCE, FHIR Group, and evaluation SQL exports.
     4→
     5→Revision ID: 44_01_export_indexes
     6→Revises: 43_01_unit_value_concept_ids
     7→Create Date: 2026-02-19 00:00:00.000000
     8→"""
     9→
    10→from typing import Sequence, Union
    11→
    12→import sqlalchemy as sa
    13→
    14→from alembic import op
    15→
    16→# revision identifiers, used by Alembic.
    17→revision: str = "44_01_export_indexes"
    18→down_revision: Union[str, None] = "43_01_unit_value_concept_ids"
    19→branch_labels: Union[str, Sequence[str], None] = None
    20→depends_on: Union[str, Sequence[str], None] = None
    21→
    22→# Index definitions: (index_name, table_name, columns)
    23→_INDEXES = [
    24→    (
    25→        "ix_atomic_domain_concept",
    26→        "atomic_criteria",
    27→        ["entity_domain", "omop_concept_id"],
    28→    ),
    29→    (
    30→        "ix_composite_proto_incl",
    31→        "composite_criteria",
    32→        ["protocol_id", "inclusion_exclusion"],
    33→    ),
    34→    (
    35→        "ix_rel_child_criterion",
    36→        "criterion_relationships",
    37→        ["child_criterion_id"],
    38→    ),
    39→    (
    40→        "ix_atomic_crit_incl",
    41→        "atomic_criteria",
    42→        ["criterion_id", "inclusion_exclusion"],
    43→    ),
    44→]
    45→
    46→
    47→def upgrade() -> None:
    48→    """Add export-optimized indexes (idempotent)."""
    49→    conn = op.get_bind()
    50→    inspector = sa.inspect(conn)
    51→
    52→    for index_name, table_name, columns in _INDEXES:
    53→        existing = {idx["name"] for idx in inspector.get_indexes(table_name)}
    54→        if index_name not in existing:
    55→            op.create_index(index_name, table_name, columns)
    56→
    57→
    58→def downgrade() -> None:
    59→    """Remove export-optimized indexes."""
    60→    for index_name, table_name, _columns in reversed(_INDEXES):
    61→        op.drop_index(index_name, table_name=table_name)
    62→
```

```
     1→"""Pydantic schemas for terminology grounding results and API candidates.
     2→
     3→These schemas define the machine-readable format for agent-to-agent communication
     4→between TerminologyRouter and MedGemma during the grounding phase.
     5→
     6→Per user decision: "Agent-to-agent communication must be machine-readable
     7→(no superfluous commentary between agents)."
     8→"""
     9→
    10→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    11→
    12→
    13→class GroundingCandidate(BaseModel):
    14→    """A single candidate match from a terminology API.
    15→
    16→    Attributes:
    17→        source_api: Name of the API that produced this candidate.
    18→        code: Terminology code (e.g. UMLS CUI "C0011847", RxNorm "6809").
    19→        preferred_term: Canonical preferred name for the concept.
    20→        semantic_type: UMLS semantic type. None for non-UMLS APIs.
    21→        score: Relevance score from the API (0.0-1.0). Higher is better.
    22→    """
    23→
    24→    source_api: str = Field(
    25→        description="Name of the terminology API that produced this candidate"
    26→    )
    27→    code: str = Field(description="Terminology code (CUI, RxNorm, ICD-10, etc.)")
    28→    preferred_term: str = Field(description="Canonical preferred name for the concept")
    29→    semantic_type: str | None = Field(
    30→        default=None,
    31→        description=(
    32→            "UMLS semantic type (e.g. 'Disease or Syndrome'). None for non-UMLS APIs."
    33→        ),
    34→    )
    35→    score: float = Field(
    36→        ge=0.0,
    37→        le=1.0,
    38→        description="Relevance score from the API (0.0-1.0). Higher is better.",
    39→    )
    40→
    41→
    42→class EntityGroundingResult(BaseModel):
    43→    """Grounding result for a single entity after MedGemma evaluation.
    44→
    45→    Contains the selected code, all candidates considered, and the reasoning
    46→    used to make the selection. Provides full audit trail per user decision:
    47→    "All agent exchanges persisted to database."
    48→
    49→    Attributes:
    50→        entity_text: Original entity text as extracted from the protocol.
    51→        entity_type: Entity type (e.g. "Medication", "Condition").
    52→        selected_code: The chosen terminology code. None if grounding failed.
    53→        selected_system: The coding system for the selected code.
    54→        preferred_term: Canonical name for the selected code.
    55→        confidence: MedGemma's confidence in the selection (0.0-1.0).
    56→        candidates: All candidates returned by terminology APIs.
    57→        reasoning: MedGemma's explanation for selecting this code.
    58→        field_mappings: Suggested AutoCriteria field mappings
    59→            (Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time). None if not yet generated.
    60→    """
    61→
    62→    entity_text: str = Field(description="Original entity text from the protocol")
    63→    entity_type: str = Field(description="Entity type (e.g. 'Medication', 'Condition')")
    64→    selected_code: str | None = Field(
    65→        default=None,
    66→        description="The chosen terminology code. None if grounding failed.",
    67→    )
    68→    selected_system: str | None = Field(
    69→        default=None,
    70→        description="The coding system for the selected code (e.g. 'UMLS').",
    71→    )
    72→    preferred_term: str | None = Field(
    73→        default=None,
    74→        description="Canonical name for the selected code.",
    75→    )
    76→    confidence: float = Field(
    77→        ge=0.0,
    78→        le=1.0,
    79→        description="MedGemma's confidence in the selection (0.0-1.0).",
    80→    )
    81→    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate] = Field(
    82→        default_factory=list,
    83→        description="All candidates returned by terminology APIs.",
    84→    )
    85→    reasoning: str = Field(
    86→        default="",
    87→        description="MedGemma's explanation for selecting this code.",
    88→    )
    89→    field_mappings: list[dict] | None = Field(
    90→        default=None,
    91→        description=(
    92→            "Suggested AutoCriteria field mappings "
    93→            "(Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time)."
    94→        ),
    95→    )
    96→    criterion_id: str | None = Field(
    97→        default=None,
    98→        description=(
    99→            "FK to Criteria record this entity belongs to (threaded from parse)."
   100→        ),
   101→    )
   102→    omop_concept_id: str | None = Field(
   103→        default=None,
   104→        description=(
   105→            "OMOP standard concept_id from Athena vocabulary lookup. "
   106→            "Used for CIRCE export and OMOP CDM joins."
   107→        ),
   108→    )
   109→    reconciliation_status: str | None = Field(
   110→        default=None,
   111→        description=(
   112→            "Dual grounding reconciliation: 'agree', 'disagreement', "
   113→            "'omop_missing', 'tooluniverse_missing'."
   114→        ),
   115→    )
   116→
   117→
   118→class GroundingBatchResult(BaseModel):
   119→    """Batch grounding result for all entities in a criteria batch.
   120→
   121→    Implements the error accumulation pattern — partial failures are preserved
   122→    alongside successes. Per user decision: "Errors accumulate — process all
   123→    entities, collect errors alongside successes (partial results are useful)."
   124→
   125→    Attributes:
   126→        results: Successfully grounded entity results.
   127→        errors: Error messages for entities that failed grounding.
   128→    """
   129→
   130→    results: list[EntityGroundingResult] = Field(
   131→        default_factory=list,
   132→        description="Successfully grounded entity results.",
   133→    )
   134→    errors: list[str] = Field(
   135→        default_factory=list,
   136→        description="Error messages for entities that failed grounding.",
   137→    )
   138→
```
