```
     1→"""Field mapping generation tool for grounded entities.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "Generate suggested field_mappings during grounding (ground node)"
     4→Per CONTEXT.md: "Criteria should be decomposed per AutoCriteria pattern: separate
     5→Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time"
     6→Per user decision: "MedGemma and Gemini collaborate: Gemini uses MedGemma as
     7→medical expert"
     8→
     9→Uses Gemini to generate suggested field mappings for the grounded entity +
    10→criterion text. These are best-effort suggestions — reviewer can edit in UI.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import logging
    16→from typing import Any
    17→
    18→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    21→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
    22→    create_structured_llm,
    23→    parse_structured_output,
    24→)
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→
    29→class FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):
    30→    """A single AutoCriteria field mapping decomposition.
    31→
    32→    Per the AutoCriteria pattern, each criterion is decomposed into
    33→    separate Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, and Time components.
    34→    """
    35→
    36→    entity: str = Field(description="The medical entity name (e.g. 'HbA1c')")
    37→    relation: str = Field(
    38→        description="The logical operator/relation (e.g. '<', '>', '=', 'has', 'is')"
    39→    )
    40→    value: str = Field(
    41→        description=(
    42→            "The specific value or threshold (e.g. '7%', 'positive', 'confirmed')"
    43→        )
    44→    )
    45→    unit: str | None = Field(
    46→        default=None,
    47→        description="Optional unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')",
    48→    )
    49→
    50→
    51→class FieldMappingResponse(BaseModel):
    52→    """Gemini structured output for field mappings."""
    53→
    54→    mappings: list[FieldMappingItem] = Field(
    55→        default_factory=list,
    56→        description="List of AutoCriteria field mapping decompositions",
    57→    )
    58→
    59→
    60→async def generate_field_mappings(
    61→    entity: EntityGroundingResult,
    62→    criterion_text: str,
    63→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    64→    """Generate suggested field mappings for a grounded entity.
    65→
    66→    Uses Gemini to decompose the criterion text into AutoCriteria field mappings:
    67→    Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time components for each discrete condition.
    68→
    69→    This is a best-effort suggestion — reviewer can edit in the UI. Errors
    70→    are logged and an empty list returned (not propagated as failures).
    71→
    72→    Args:
    73→        entity: Grounded EntityGroundingResult with code and preferred term.
    74→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
    75→
    76→    Returns:
    77→        List of field mapping dicts with keys: entity, relation, value, unit.
    78→        Empty list if generation fails.
    79→    """
    80→    if not criterion_text:
    81→        return []
    82→
    83→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)
    84→    if structured_llm is None:
    85→        return []
    86→
    87→    try:
    88→        # Build a context-rich prompt for field mapping generation
    89→        grounded_term = entity.preferred_term or entity.entity_text
    90→        code_context = ""
    91→        if entity.selected_code and entity.selected_system:
    92→            system = entity.selected_system.upper()
    93→            code_context = f"(grounded to {system} code: {entity.selected_code})"
    94→
    95→        prompt = (
    96→            "You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. Decompose the"
    97→            " following criterion into structured AutoCriteria field"
    98→            " mappings using the Entity-Relation-Value-Unit pattern.\n\n"
    99→            f"Medical entity: {grounded_term} {code_context}\n"
   100→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n\n"
   101→            "Instructions:\n"
   102→            "- Extract each discrete measurement, threshold, or condition"
   103→            " as a separate mapping\n"
   104→            "- entity: the specific measurement or concept"
   105→            " (e.g. 'HbA1c', 'Age', 'eGFR')\n"
   106→            "- relation: the logical operator"
   107→            " (<, >, >=, <=, =, has, is, within, not)\n"
   108→            "- value: the specific threshold or value"
   109→            " (e.g. '7', 'positive', 'confirmed')\n"
   110→            "- unit: the unit if applicable (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')"
   111→            " or null\n"
   112→            "- Create one mapping per discrete condition in the criterion\n"
   113→            "- If no clear measurement exists, create one mapping with"
   114→            " relation='has', value='confirmed'"
   115→        )
   116→
   117→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   118→        response = parse_structured_output(result, FieldMappingResponse)
   119→
   120→        mappings = [
   121→            {
   122→                "entity": m.entity,
   123→                "relation": m.relation,
   124→                "value": m.value,
   125→                "unit": m.unit,
   126→                "entity_concept_id": entity.selected_code,
   127→                "entity_concept_system": entity.selected_system,
   128→                "omop_concept_id": entity.omop_concept_id,
   129→                "entity_type": entity.entity_type,
   130→            }
   131→            for m in response.mappings
   132→        ]
   133→
   134→        logger.info(
   135→            "Generated %d field mapping(s) for entity '%s'",
   136→            len(mappings),
   137→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   138→        )
   139→        return mappings
   140→
   141→    except Exception as e:
   142→        logger.warning(
   143→            "Field mapping generation failed for entity '%s': %s",
   144→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   145→            e,
   146→            exc_info=True,
   147→        )
   148→        # Best-effort: return empty list on failure
   149→        return []
   150→

```
     1→"""Field mapping generation tool for grounded entities.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "Generate suggested field_mappings during grounding (ground node)"
     4→Per CONTEXT.md: "Criteria should be decomposed per AutoCriteria pattern: separate
     5→Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time"
     6→Per user decision: "MedGemma and Gemini collaborate: Gemini uses MedGemma as
     7→medical expert"
     8→
     9→Uses Gemini to generate suggested field mappings for the grounded entity +
    10→criterion text. These are best-effort suggestions — reviewer can edit in UI.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import logging
    16→from typing import Any, Literal
    17→
    18→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    21→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
    22→    create_structured_llm,
    23→    parse_structured_output,
    24→)
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→# Mapping from legacy/LLM relation operators to the frontend's RelationOperator set
    29→_RELATION_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    30→    "has": "contains",
    31→    "is": "=",
    32→    "not": "not_contains",
    33→    "==": "=",
    34→    "range": "within",
    35→}
    36→
    37→
    38→def _normalize_relation(rel: str) -> str:
    39→    """Normalize a relation operator to the frontend's accepted set.
    40→
    41→    Maps legacy operators (has, is, not, ==, range) to the standard set:
    42→    =, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within, not_in_last, contains, not_contains.
    43→    """
    44→    return _RELATION_MAP.get(rel, rel)
    45→
    46→
    47→class FieldMappingValue(BaseModel):
    48→    """Typed value object for field mappings with type discriminator.
    49→
    50→    Supports three value shapes:
    51→    - standard: single value + unit (e.g. HbA1c < 7%)
    52→    - range: min/max + unit (e.g. Age 18-65 years)
    53→    - temporal: duration + unit (e.g. within 6 months)
    54→    """
    55→
    56→    type: Literal["standard", "range", "temporal"] = Field(
    57→        description="Value type discriminator"
    58→    )
    59→    value: str | None = Field(
    60→        default=None, description="Value for standard type (e.g. '7')"
    61→    )
    62→    unit: str | None = Field(
    63→        default=None, description="Unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'months')"
    64→    )
    65→    min: str | None = Field(
    66→        default=None, description="Minimum value for range type"
    67→    )
    68→    max: str | None = Field(
    69→        default=None, description="Maximum value for range type"
    70→    )
    71→    duration: str | None = Field(
    72→        default=None, description="Duration value for temporal type"
    73→    )
    74→
    75→
    76→class FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):
    77→    """A single AutoCriteria field mapping decomposition.
    78→
    79→    Per the AutoCriteria pattern, each criterion is decomposed into
    80→    separate Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, and Time components.
    81→    """
    82→
    83→    entity: str = Field(description="The medical entity name (e.g. 'HbA1c')")
    84→    relation: str = Field(
    85→        description="The logical operator/relation (e.g. '<', '>', '=', 'contains')"
    86→    )
    87→    value: FieldMappingValue = Field(
    88→        description="Typed value object with type discriminator"
    89→    )
    90→    unit: str | None = Field(
    91→        default=None,
    92→        description="Optional unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')",
    93→    )
    94→
    95→
    96→class FieldMappingResponse(BaseModel):
    97→    """Gemini structured output for field mappings."""
    98→
    99→    mappings: list[FieldMappingItem] = Field(
   100→        default_factory=list,
   101→        description="List of AutoCriteria field mapping decompositions",
   102→    )
   103→
   104→
   105→async def generate_field_mappings(
   106→    entity: EntityGroundingResult,
   107→    criterion_text: str,
   108→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   109→    """Generate suggested field mappings for a grounded entity.
   110→
   111→    Uses Gemini to decompose the criterion text into AutoCriteria field mappings:
   112→    Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time components for each discrete condition.
   113→
   114→    This is a best-effort suggestion — reviewer can edit in the UI. Errors
   115→    are logged and an empty list returned (not propagated as failures).
   116→
   117→    Args:
   118→        entity: Grounded EntityGroundingResult with code and preferred term.
   119→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   120→
   121→    Returns:
   122→        List of field mapping dicts with keys: entity, relation, value,
   123→        entity_code, entity_system, omop_concept_id, entity_type.
   124→        Empty list if generation fails.
   125→    """
   126→    if not criterion_text:
   127→        return []
   128→
   129→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)
   130→    if structured_llm is None:
   131→        return []
   132→
   133→    try:
   134→        # Build a context-rich prompt for field mapping generation
   135→        grounded_term = entity.preferred_term or entity.entity_text
   136→        code_context = ""
   137→        if entity.selected_code and entity.selected_system:
   138→            system = entity.selected_system.upper()
   139→            code_context = f"(grounded to {system} code: {entity.selected_code})"
   140→
   141→        prompt = (
   142→            "You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. Decompose the"
   143→            " following criterion into structured AutoCriteria field"
   144→            " mappings using the Entity-Relation-Value-Unit pattern.\n\n"
   145→            f"Medical entity: {grounded_term} {code_context}\n"
   146→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n\n"
   147→            "Instructions:\n"
   148→            "- Extract each discrete measurement, threshold, or condition"
   149→            " as a separate mapping\n"
   150→            "- entity: the specific measurement or concept"
   151→            " (e.g. 'HbA1c', 'Age', 'eGFR')\n"
   152→            "- relation: MUST be one of: =, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within,"
   153→            " not_in_last, contains, not_contains\n"
   154→            '- value: a typed object with a "type" discriminator:\n'
   155→            '  - Standard: {"type": "standard", "value": "7", "unit": "%"}\n'
   156→            '  - Range: {"type": "range", "min": "18", "max": "65",'
   157→            ' "unit": "years"}\n'
   158→            '  - Temporal: {"type": "temporal", "duration": "6",'
   159→            ' "unit": "months"}\n'
   160→            "- Create one mapping per discrete condition in the criterion\n"
   161→            "- If no clear measurement exists, create one mapping with"
   162→            " relation='contains',"
   163→            ' value={"type": "standard", "value": "confirmed", "unit": ""}'
   164→        )
   165→
   166→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   167→        response = parse_structured_output(result, FieldMappingResponse)
   168→
   169→        mappings = [
   170→            {
   171→                "entity": m.entity,
   172→                "relation": _normalize_relation(m.relation),
   173→                "value": m.value.model_dump(exclude_none=True),
   174→                "entity_code": entity.selected_code,
   175→                "entity_system": entity.selected_system,
   176→                "omop_concept_id": entity.omop_concept_id,
   177→                "entity_type": entity.entity_type,
   178→            }
   179→            for m in response.mappings
   180→        ]
   181→
   182→        logger.info(
   183→            "Generated %d field mapping(s) for entity '%s'",
   184→            len(mappings),
   185→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   186→        )
   187→        return mappings
   188→
   189→    except Exception as e:
   190→        logger.warning(
   191→            "Field mapping generation failed for entity '%s': %s",
   192→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   193→            e,
   194→            exc_info=True,
   195→        )
   196→        # Best-effort: return empty list on failure
   197→        return []
   198→
```

  "old_string": "/**\n * Build initial form values for the structured editor from a criterion's\n * AI-extracted data (numeric_thresholds, temporal_constraint, conditions).\n */\nexport function buildInitialValues(criterion: Criterion): StructuredFieldFormValues {\n    // Priority 1: existing field_mappings from a previous structured edit\n    const cond = criterion.conditions as Record<string, unknown> | null;\n    if (cond && 'field_mappings' in cond && Array.isArray(cond.field_mappings)) {\n        const fms = cond.field_mappings as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;\n        const mappings: FieldMapping[] = fms.map((fm) => {\n            const rel = (fm.relation as string) ?? '';\n            const rawVal = fm.value as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;\n            let value: FieldValue = { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' };\n            if (rawVal && rawVal.type === 'range') {\n                value = {\n                    type: 'range',\n                    min: String(rawVal.min ?? ''),\n                    max: String(rawVal.max ?? ''),\n                    unit: String(rawVal.unit ?? ''),\n                };\n            } else if (rawVal && rawVal.type === 'temporal') {\n                value = {\n                    type: 'temporal',\n                    duration: String(rawVal.duration ?? ''),\n                    unit: (rawVal.unit as TemporalUnit) ?? 'days',\n                };\n            } else if (rawVal && rawVal.type === 'standard') {\n                value = {\n                    type: 'standard',\n                    value: String(rawVal.value ?? ''),\n                    unit: String(rawVal.unit ?? ''),\n                };\n            }\n            return {\n                entity: String(fm.entity ?? ''),\n                entity_code: fm.entity_code ? String(fm.entity_code) : undefined,\n                entity_system: fm.entity_system ? String(fm.entity_system) : undefined,\n                relation: (rel as RelationOperator) || '',\n                value,\n            };\n        });\n        if (mappings.length > 0) return { mappings };\n    }",
  "new_string": "/** Valid frontend relation operators. */\nconst VALID_RELATIONS = new Set<string>([\n    '=', '!=', '>', '>=', '<', '<=', 'within', 'not_in_last', 'contains', 'not_contains',\n]);\n\n/** Normalize a raw relation string to a valid RelationOperator or empty. */\nexport function normalizeRelation(raw: string): RelationOperator | '' {\n    const map: Record<string, RelationOperator> = {\n        has: 'contains',\n        is: '=',\n        not: 'not_contains',\n        '==': '=',\n        range: 'within',\n    };\n    const mapped = map[raw] ?? raw;\n    return VALID_RELATIONS.has(mapped) ? (mapped as RelationOperator) : '';\n}\n\n/** Normalize a field mapping's value to a typed FieldValue. */\nexport function normalizeFieldValue(fm: Record<string, unknown>): FieldValue {\n    const rawVal = fm.value;\n\n    // New typed format: value is an object with a 'type' key\n    if (rawVal && typeof rawVal === 'object' && !Array.isArray(rawVal)) {\n        const obj = rawVal as Record<string, unknown>;\n        if (obj.type === 'range') {\n            return {\n                type: 'range',\n                min: String(obj.min ?? ''),\n                max: String(obj.max ?? ''),\n                unit: String(obj.unit ?? ''),\n            };\n        }\n        if (obj.type === 'temporal') {\n            return {\n                type: 'temporal',\n                duration: String(obj.duration ?? ''),\n                unit: (obj.unit as TemporalUnit) ?? 'days',\n            };\n        }\n        if (obj.type === 'standard') {\n            return {\n                type: 'standard',\n                value: String(obj.value ?? ''),\n                unit: String(obj.unit ?? ''),\n            };\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Legacy flat format: value is a string or number\n    if (typeof rawVal === 'string' || typeof rawVal === 'number') {\n        return {\n            type: 'standard',\n            value: String(rawVal),\n            unit: String(fm.unit ?? ''),\n        };\n    }\n\n    return { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' };\n}\n\n/**\n * Build initial form values for the structured editor from a criterion's\n * AI-extracted data (numeric_thresholds, temporal_constraint, conditions).\n */\nexport function buildInitialValues(criterion: Criterion): StructuredFieldFormValues {\n    // Priority 1: existing field_mappings from a previous structured edit\n    const cond = criterion.conditions as Record<string, unknown> | null;\n    if (cond && 'field_mappings' in cond && Array.isArray(cond.field_mappings)) {\n        const fms = cond.field_mappings as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;\n        const mappings: FieldMapping[] = fms.map((fm) => {\n            const rel = (fm.relation as string) ?? '';\n            const value = normalizeFieldValue(fm);\n            return {\n                entity: String(fm.entity ?? ''),\n                entity_code: (fm.entity_code ?? fm.entity_concept_id)\n                    ? String(fm.entity_code ?? fm.entity_concept_id)\n                    : undefined,\n                entity_system: (fm.entity_system ?? fm.entity_concept_system)\n                    ? String(fm.entity_system ?? fm.entity_concept_system)\n                    : undefined,\n                omop_concept_id: fm.omop_concept_id\n                    ? String(fm.omop_concept_id)\n                    : undefined,\n                relation: normalizeRelation(rel),\n                value,\n            };\n        });\n        if (mappings.length > 0) return { mappings };\n    }"
}

{

  "old_string": "import type { Criterion } from '../hooks/useReviews';\n\ninterface FieldMappingBadgesProps {\n    criterion: Criterion;\n    onEditClick?: () => void;\n}\n\nfunction formatMappingValue(value: Record<string, unknown>): string {\n    if (typeof value !== 'object') return '';\n    if (value.type === 'range')\n        return `${value.min}–${value.max}${value.unit ? ` ${value.unit}` : ''}`;\n    if (value.type === 'temporal') return `${value.duration} ${value.unit}`;\n    if (value.type === 'standard') return `${value.value}${value.unit ? ` ${value.unit}` : ''}`;\n    return '';\n}\n\nfunction renderValue(value: unknown): string {\n    if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';\n    if (typeof value === 'string') return value;\n    if (typeof value === 'number') return String(value);\n    if (typeof value === 'object') return formatMappingValue(value as Record<string, unknown>);\n    return '';\n}\n\nexport default function FieldMappingBadges({ criterion, onEditClick }: FieldMappingBadgesProps) {\n    const cond = criterion.conditions as Record<string, unknown> | null;\n    const fieldMappings =\n        cond && 'field_mappings' in cond && Array.isArray(cond.field_mappings)\n            ? (cond.field_mappings as Array<{\n                  entity: string;\n                  entity_code?: string;\n                  entity_system?: string;\n                  relation: string;\n                  value: unknown;\n              }>)\n            : null;\n\n    if (!fieldMappings || fieldMappings.length === 0) return null;\n\n    const badgeContent = (mapping: (typeof fieldMappings)[number]) => {\n        const valueText = renderValue(mapping.value);\n        return (\n            <>\n                <span className=\"font-semibold text-blue-900\">{mapping.entity || '—'}</span>\n                {mapping.entity_code && (\n                    <span className=\"inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-green-50 border border-green-200 px-1.5 py-0 text-[10px] text-green-700\">\n                        {mapping.entity_system?.toUpperCase()}: {mapping.entity_code}\n                    </span>\n                )}\n                {mapping.relation && (\n                    <span className=\"text-blue-600 font-mono text-xs\">{mapping.relation}</span>\n                )}\n                {mapping.relation && valueText ? (\n                    <span className=\"text-blue-800\">{valueText}</span>\n                ) : mapping.relation && !valueText ? (\n                    <span className=\"text-muted-foreground/50 italic text-xs\">no value</span>\n                ) : null}\n            </>\n        );\n    };",
  "new_string": "import type { Criterion } from '../hooks/useReviews';\n\ninterface FieldMappingBadgesProps {\n    criterion: Criterion;\n    onEditClick?: () => void;\n}\n\n/** Mapping from legacy relation operators to frontend-standard operators. */\nconst RELATION_DISPLAY_MAP: Record<string, string> = {\n    has: 'contains',\n    is: '=',\n    not: 'not_contains',\n    '==': '=',\n    range: 'within',\n};\n\nfunction normalizeRelationDisplay(rel: string): string {\n    return RELATION_DISPLAY_MAP[rel] ?? rel;\n}\n\nfunction formatMappingValue(value: Record<string, unknown>): string {\n    if (typeof value !== 'object') return '';\n    if (value.type === 'range')\n        return `${value.min}–${value.max}${value.unit ? ` ${value.unit}` : ''}`;\n    if (value.type === 'temporal') return `${value.duration} ${value.unit}`;\n    if (value.type === 'standard') return `${value.value}${value.unit ? ` ${value.unit}` : ''}`;\n    return '';\n}\n\nfunction renderValue(value: unknown): string {\n    if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';\n    if (typeof value === 'string') return value;\n    if (typeof value === 'number') return String(value);\n    if (typeof value === 'object') return formatMappingValue(value as Record<string, unknown>);\n    return '';\n}\n\ninterface RawFieldMapping {\n    entity: string;\n    entity_code?: string;\n    entity_concept_id?: string;\n    entity_system?: string;\n    entity_concept_system?: string;\n    omop_concept_id?: string;\n    relation: string;\n    value: unknown;\n    unit?: string;\n}\n\nexport default function FieldMappingBadges({ criterion, onEditClick }: FieldMappingBadgesProps) {\n    const cond = criterion.conditions as Record<string, unknown> | null;\n    const fieldMappings =\n        cond && 'field_mappings' in cond && Array.isArray(cond.field_mappings)\n            ? (cond.field_mappings as Array<RawFieldMapping>)\n            : null;\n\n    if (!fieldMappings || fieldMappings.length === 0) return null;\n\n    const badgeContent = (mapping: RawFieldMapping) => {\n        // Legacy key fallbacks\n        const entityCode = mapping.entity_code ?? mapping.entity_concept_id;\n        const entitySystem = mapping.entity_system ?? mapping.entity_concept_system;\n        const relation = normalizeRelationDisplay(mapping.relation);\n\n        // Handle flat value format (string/number) alongside typed object format\n        let valueText: string;\n        if (typeof mapping.value === 'string' || typeof mapping.value === 'number') {\n            const unit = mapping.unit ? ` ${mapping.unit}` : '';\n            valueText = `${mapping.value}${unit}`;\n        } else {\n            valueText = renderValue(mapping.value);\n        }\n\n        return (\n            <>\n                <span className=\"font-semibold text-blue-900\">{mapping.entity || '—'}</span>\n                {entityCode && (\n                    <span className=\"inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-green-50 border border-green-200 px-1.5 py-0 text-[10px] text-green-700\">\n                        {entitySystem?.toUpperCase()}: {entityCode}\n                    </span>\n                )}\n                {mapping.omop_concept_id && (\n                    <span className=\"inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 px-1.5 py-0 text-[10px] text-amber-700\">\n                        OMOP: {mapping.omop_concept_id}\n                    </span>\n                )}\n                {relation && (\n                    <span className=\"text-blue-600 font-mono text-xs\">{relation}</span>\n                )}\n                {relation && valueText ? (\n                    <span className=\"text-blue-800\">{valueText}</span>\n                ) : relation && !valueText ? (\n                    <span className=\"text-muted-foreground/50 italic text-xs\">no value</span>\n                ) : null}\n            </>\n        );\n    };"
}

'';\n        const upperVal = t.upper_value as number | null;\n\n        if (val === null || val === undefined) continue;\n\n        // Find the next unused measurable entity\n        let matchedEntity: (typeof measurableEntities)[number] | undefined;\n        for (let j = 0; j < measurableEntities.length; j++) {\n            if (!usedEntityIndices.has(j)) {\n                matchedEntity = measurableEntities[j];\n                usedEntityIndices.add(j);\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        const entity = matchedEntity ? entityLabel(matchedEntity) : '';\n        const entityCode = matchedEntity?.snomed_code ?? matchedEntity?.umls_cui ?? undefined;\n        const entitySystem = matchedEntity?.snomed_code\n            ? {
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/.REDACTED.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Normalize field_mappings in criteria.conditions to match frontend schema.\n\nWraps flat string values into typed objects, renames entity_concept_id/\nentity_concept_system to entity_code/entity_system, and normalizes\nrelation operators (has->contains, is->=, not->not_contains).\n\nRevision ID: 45_01_normalize_field_mappings\nRevises: 44_01_export_indexes\nCreate Date: 2026-02-23 00:00:00.000000\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nfrom typing import Sequence, Union\n\nimport sqlalchemy as sa\n\nfrom alembic import op\n\n# revision identifiers, used by Alembic.\nrevision: str = \"45_01_normalize_field_mappings\"\ndown_revision: Union[str, None] = \"44_01_export_indexes\"\nbranch_labels: Union[str, Sequence[str], None] = None\ndepends_on: Union[str, Sequence[str], None] = None\n\n# Legacy relation operators that need normalization\n_RELATION_MAP = {\n    \"has\": \"contains\",\n    \"is\": \"=\",\n    \"not\": \"not_contains\",\n    \"==\": \"=\",\n    \"range\": \"within\",\n}\n\n\ndef _normalize_mapping(mapping: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Normalize a single field mapping dict (idempotent).\"\"\"\n    result = dict(mapping)\n\n    # Rename entity_concept_id -> entity_code (if not already renamed)\n    if \"entity_concept_id\" in result and \"entity_code\" not in result:\n        result[\"entity_code\"] = result.pop(\"entity_concept_id\")\n    elif \"entity_concept_id\" in result:\n        del result[\"entity_concept_id\"]\n\n    # Rename entity_concept_system -> entity_system (if not already renamed)\n    if \"entity_concept_system\" in result and \"entity_system\" not in result:\n        result[\"entity_system\"] = result.pop(\"entity_concept_system\")\n    elif \"entity_concept_system\" in result:\n        del result[\"entity_concept_system\"]\n\n    # Normalize relation\n    rel = result.get(\"relation\", \"\")\n    if isinstance(rel, str) and rel in _RELATION_MAP:\n        result[\"relation\"] = _RELATION_MAP[rel]\n\n    # Wrap flat value into typed object (idempotent: skip if already dict)\n    value = result.get(\"value\")\n    if not isinstance(value, dict):\n        unit = result.pop(\"unit\", None) or \"\"\n        result[\"value\"] = {\n            \"type\": \"standard\",\n            \"value\": str(value) if value is not None else \"\",\n            \"unit\": str(unit),\n        }\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef upgrade() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Normalize existing field_mappings in criteria.conditions.\"\"\"\n    conn = op.get_bind()\n\n    rows = conn.execute(\n        sa.text(\n            \"SELECT id, conditions FROM criteria \"\n            \"WHERE conditions IS NOT NULL \"\n            \"AND conditions::text LIKE '%field_mappings%'\"\n        )\n    ).fetchall()\n\n    for row in rows:\n        row_id = row[0]\n        conditions = row[1]\n        if isinstance(conditions, str):\n            conditions = json.loads(conditions)\n\n        field_mappings = conditions.get(\"field_mappings\")\n        if not isinstance(field_mappings, list):\n            continue\n\n        normalized = [_normalize_mapping(m) for m in field_mappings]\n        conditions[\"field_mappings\"] = normalized\n\n        conn.execute(\n            sa.text(\"UPDATE criteria SET conditions = :cond WHERE id = :id\"),\n            {\"cond\": json.dumps(conditions), \"id\": row_id},\n        )\n\n\ndef downgrade() -> None:\n    \"\"\"No-op: cannot reconstruct original flat format reliably.\"\"\"\n    pass\n"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"Tests for field_mapper output format normalization.\n\nVerifies that _normalize_relation() maps legacy operators correctly and\nthat generate_field_mappings() produces the expected typed value format\nwith correct key names.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom unittest.mock import AsyncMock, patch\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper import (\n    FieldMappingItem,\n    FieldMappingResponse,\n    FieldMappingValue,\n    _normalize_relation,\n    generate_field_mappings,\n)\nfrom protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult\n\n\n# --- _normalize_relation tests ---\n\n\nclass TestNormalizeRelation:\n    \"\"\"Test the _normalize_relation() mapping function.\"\"\"\n\n    def test_has_maps_to_contains(self) -> None:\n        assert _normalize_relation(\"has\") == \"contains\"\n\n    def test_is_maps_to_equals(self) -> None:\n        assert _normalize_relation(\"is\") == \"=\"\n\n    def test_not_maps_to_not_contains(self) -> None:\n        assert _normalize_relation(\"not\") == \"not_contains\"\n\n    def test_double_equals_maps_to_equals(self) -> None:\n        assert _normalize_relation(\"==\") == \"=\"\n\n    def test_range_maps_to_within(self) -> None:\n        assert _normalize_relation(\"range\") == \"within\"\n\n    def test_passthrough_standard_operators(self) -> None:\n        for op in [\"=\", \"!=\", \">\", \">=\", \"<\", \"<=\", \"within\", \"not_in_last\",\n                    \"contains\", \"not_contains\"]:\n            assert _normalize_relation(op) == op\n\n    def test_passthrough_unknown_operator(self) -> None:\n        assert _normalize_relation(\"custom_op\") == \"custom_op\"\n\n    def test_empty_string_passthrough(self) -> None:\n        assert _normalize_relation(\"\") == \"\"\n\n\n# --- generate_field_mappings output format tests ---\n\n\ndef _make_entity(**overrides: object) -> EntityGroundingResult:\n    \"\"\"Create a minimal EntityGroundingResult for testing.\"\"\"\n    defaults = {\n        \"entity_text\": \"HbA1c\",\n        \"entity_type\": \"Lab_Value\",\n        \"selected_code\": \"C0019018\",\n        \"selected_system\": \"umls\",\n        \"preferred_term\": \"Hemoglobin A1c\",\n        \"confidence\": 0.95,\n        \"omop_concept_id\": \"3004410\",\n    }\n    defaults.update(overrides)\n    return EntityGroundingResult(**defaults)\n\n\ndef _make_mapping_response(*items: FieldMappingItem) -> FieldMappingResponse:\n    return FieldMappingResponse(mappings=list(items))\n\n\nclass TestGenerateFieldMappingsFormat:\n    \"\"\"Test that generate_field_mappings produces correctly formatted output.\"\"\"\n\n    @pytest.mark.asyncio\n    async def test_output_has_typed_value_object(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Value should be a dict with 'type' discriminator, not a flat string.\"\"\"\n        entity = _make_entity()\n        response = _make_mapping_response(\n            FieldMappingItem(\n                entity=\"HbA1c\",\n                relation=\"<\",\n                value=FieldMappingValue(type=\"standard\", value=\"7\", unit=\"%\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n        with patch(\n            \"protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper.create_structured_llm\"\n        ) as mock_llm_factory, patch(\n            \"protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper.parse_structured_output\",\n            return_value=response,\n        ):\n            mock_llm = AsyncMock()\n            mock_llm_factory.return_value = mock_llm\n\n            result = await generate_field_mappings(entity, \"HbA1c < 7%\")\n\n        assert len(result) == 1\n        mapping = result[0]\n        assert isinstance(mapping[\"value\"], dict)\n        assert mapping[\"value\"][\"type\"] == \"standard\"\n        assert mapping[\"value\"][\"value\"] == \"7\"\n        assert mapping[\"value\"][\"unit\"] == \"%\"\n\n    @pytest.mark.asyncio\n    async def test_output_has_correct_key_names(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Should use entity_code/entity_system, not entity_concept_id.\"\"\"\n        entity = _make_entity()\n        response = _make_mapping_response(\n            FieldMappingItem(\n                entity=\"HbA1c\",\n                relation=\"<\",\n                value=FieldMappingValue(type=\"standard\", value=\"7\", unit=\"%\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n        with patch(\n            \"protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper.create_structured_llm\"\n        ) as mock_llm_factory, patch(\n            \"protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper.parse_structured_output\",\n            return_value=response,\n        ):\n            mock_llm = AsyncMock()\n            mock_llm_factory.return_value = mock_llm\n\n            result = await generate_field_mappings(entity, \"HbA1c < 7%\")\n\n        mapping = result[0]\n        assert \"entity_code\" in mapping\n        assert \"entity_system\" in mapping\n        assert \"entity_concept_id\" not in mapping\n        assert \"entity_concept_system\" not in mapping\n        assert mapping[\"entity_code\"] == \"C0019018\"\n        assert mapping[\"entity_system\"] == \"umls\"\n\n    @pytest.mark.asyncio\n    async def test_output_includes_omop_concept_id(self) -> None:\n        entity = _make_entity(omop_concept_id=\"3004410\")\n        response = _make_mapping_response(\n            FieldMappingItem(\n                entity=\"HbA1c\",\n                relation=\"<\",\n                value=FieldMappingValue(type=\"standard\", value=\"7\", unit=\"%\"),\n            )\n        )\n\n        with patch(\n 7%\")\n\n        assert result[0][\"omop_concept_id\"] == \"3004410\"\n\n    @pytest.mark.asyncio\n    async def test_relation_is_normalized(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Legacy relation 'has' should be normalized to 'contains'.\"\"\"\n        entity = _make_entity()\n        response = _make_mapping_response(\n            FieldMappingItem(\n                entity=\"Diabetes\",\n                relation=\"has\",\n                value=FieldMappingValue(\n                    type=\"standard\", value=\"confirmed\", unit=\"\"\n                ),\n            )\n        )\n\n        with \"protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper.parse_structured_output\",\n            return_value=response,\n        ):\n            mock_llm = AsyncMock()\n            mock_llm_factory.return_value = mock_llm\n\n            result = await generate_field_mappings(entity, \"Has diabetes\")\n\n        assert result[0][\"relation\"] == \"contains\"\n\n    @pytest.mark.asyncio\n    async def test_range_value_excludes_none_fields(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"model_dump(exclude_none=True) should omit unused fields.\"\"\"\n        entity = _make_entity()\n        response = All four phases are complete. Here's a summary:

## Summary

**4 commits on `claude/busy-knuth`:**

| Phase | Commit | Description |
|-------|--------|-------------|
| **A** | `bbbbe81` | Backend normalization: `FieldMappingValue` model, `_normalize_relation()`, updated Gemini prompt, renamed keys |
| **B** | `b0ef4f3` | Frontend hardening: `normalizeFieldValue()`, `normalizeRelation()`, key fallbacks, OMOP badges, Priority 2 fix |
| **C** | `a55f659` | Data migration: Alembic migration normalizing existing `field_mappings` in `criteria.conditions` |
| **D** | `3dbe297` | Tests: 15 backend + 24 frontend tests, all passing |

**Root causes addressed:**
- **RC1** (flat values): Backend now produces typed objects; frontend handles both formats
- **RC2** (relation mismatch): Both sides normalize `has`/`is`/`not`/`==`/`range` to valid operators
- **RC3** (key naming): Backend uses `entity_code`/`entity_system`; frontend falls back to legacy keys
- **RC4** (OMOP invisible): `omop_concept_id` added to types, passed through, displayed as amber badge
- **RC5** (index-based matching): Replaced with `usedEntityIndices` Set, remaining entities appended

```json
{
  "command": "gh pr create --title \"fix: normalize structured field mappings between backend and frontend\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n\nFixes the structured fields modification panel in the HITL review UI, which was showing raw text instead of grounded SNOMED/UMLS concepts, missing relations and values, and invisible OMOP concept IDs.\n\n**Root causes addressed (see `INVESTIGATION_REPORT.md` for full analysis):**\n\n- **RC1 — Flat values vs typed objects:** Backend `field_mapper.py` produced flat string values (`\"value\": \"6\"`) but the frontend `buildInitialValues` expected typed objects (`{\"type\": \"standard\", \"value\": \"6\", \"unit\": \"%\"}`). All AI-generated values were silently dropped.\n- **RC2 — Relation operator mismatch:** Backend emitted `has`/`is`/`not` but frontend only accepted `=`/`!=`/`>`/`>=`/`<`/`<=`/`within`/`not_in_last`/`contains`/`not_contains`. Unrecognized operators caused empty dropdowns and hidden value fields.\n- **RC3 — Key naming mismatch:** Backend stored `entity_concept_id`/`entity_concept_system` but frontend read `entity_code`/`entity_system`. Grounding badges never appeared.\n- **RC4 — OMOP concepts invisible:** `omop_concept_id` was stored in the DB but absent from TypeScript types and UI components.\n- **RC5 — Index-based entity-threshold matching:** Priority 2 fallback path matched entities to thresholds by array index, causing misalignment when counts differed.\n\n## Changes\n\n### Phase A — Backend normalization (`field_mapper.py`)\n- Added `FieldMappingValue` Pydantic model with `type` discriminator (`standard`/`range`/`temporal`)\n- Added `_normalize_relation()` mapping legacy operators to the frontend's valid set\n- Updated Gemini prompt to instruct typed value objects and restrict relation operators\n- Renamed `entity_concept_id` → `entity_code`, `entity_concept_system` → `entity_system`\n\n### Phase B — Frontend hardening\n- Added `normalizeFieldValue()` helper handling both typed objects and legacy flat strings\n- Added `normalizeRelation()` helper with the same mapping as backend\n- Added fallback key lookups (`entity_code ?? entity_concept_id`) for backward compatibility\n- Added `omop_concept_id` to `FieldMapping` interfaces in `types.ts` and `useReviews.ts`\n- Added amber OMOP badge in `FieldMappingBadges.tsx` and `StructuredFieldEditor.tsx`\n- Replaced index-based entity-threshold matching with `usedEntityIndices` Set approach\n\n### Phase C — Data migration\n- Alembic migration `45_01_normalize_field_mappings` that normalizes existing `field_mappings` in `criteria.conditions` (wraps flat values, renames keys, normalizes relations). Idempotent.\n\n### Phase D — Tests\n- **Backend:** 15 tests for `_normalize_relation` mappings and `generate_field_mappings` output format\n- **Frontend:** 24 tests for `normalizeRelation`, `normalizeFieldValue`, `buildInitialValues` (Priority 1 + Priority 2 paths)\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [ ] Backend tests: `cd services/protocol-processor-service && uv run pytest tests/tools/test_field_mapper_format.py -v` (15 passing)\n- [ ] Frontend tests: `cd apps/hitl-ui && npx vitest run src/components/__tests__/buildInitialValues.test.ts` (24 passing)\n- [ ] TypeScript check: `cd apps/hitl-ui && npx tsc --noEmit`\n- [ ] Lint: `uv run ruff check services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py`\n- [ ] Manual E2E: Process a protocol → open review UI → verify entity badges, relation dropdowns, values, and OMOP badges display correctly\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Create PR with detailed description",
  "timeout": 30000
}
```

     1→"""Tests for Phase 1b changes: concept ID wiring, criterion_id lookup, accumulation.
     2→
     3→Covers:
     4→1. Field mapping concept ID enrichment (field_mapper.py)
     5→2. Criterion ID-based direct lookup (persist.py)
     6→3. Field mapping accumulation (persist.py)
     7→4. EntityGroundingResult schema fields (grounding.py)
     8→
     9→All DB interactions are mocked -- no live database required.
    10→"""
    11→
    12→from __future__ import annotations
    13→
    14→import os
    15→from typing import Any
    16→from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock, patch
    17→
    18→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    19→from protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper import (
    20→    FieldMappingItem,
    21→    FieldMappingResponse,
    22→    generate_field_mappings,
    23→)
    24→
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→# Helpers
    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28→
    29→
    30→def _make_entity(**overrides: Any) -> EntityGroundingResult:
    31→    """Build an EntityGroundingResult with sensible defaults."""
    32→    defaults: dict[str, Any] = {
    33→        "entity_text": "type 2 diabetes",
    34→        "entity_type": "Condition",
    35→        "selected_code": None,
    36→        "selected_system": None,
    37→        "preferred_term": None,
    38→        "confidence": 0.9,
    39→        "candidates": [],
    40→        "reasoning": "",
    41→    }
    42→    defaults.update(overrides)
    43→    return EntityGroundingResult(**defaults)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _make_criteria_mock(**overrides: Any) -> MagicMock:
    47→    """Build a mock Criteria record with mutable .conditions."""
    48→    mock = MagicMock()
    49→    mock.id = overrides.get("id", "crit-001")
    50→    mock.batch_id = overrides.get("batch_id", "batch-001")
    51→    mock.text = overrides.get("text", "HbA1c < 7%")
    52→    mock.conditions = overrides.get("conditions", None)
    53→    return mock
    54→
    55→
    56→# ===========================================================================
    57→# TestFieldMappingConceptIds
    58→# ===========================================================================
    59→
    60→
    61→class TestFieldMappingConceptIds:
    62→    """Test that field_mapper includes concept IDs in mapping dicts."""
    63→
    64→    @patch.dict(os.environ, {"GOOGLE_API_KEY": "test-key"})
    65→    @patch("langchain_google_genai.ChatGoogleGenerativeAI")
    66→    async def test_mappings_include_concept_ids(self, mock_chat_cls: MagicMock) -> None:
    67→        """Mapping dicts should contain all three concept ID keys."""
    68→        response = FieldMappingResponse(
    69→            mappings=[
    70→                FieldMappingItem(entity="HbA1c", relation="<", value="7", unit="%")
    71→            ]
    72→        )
    73→        mock_chain = MagicMock()
    74→        mock_chain.ainvoke = AsyncMock(return_value=response)
    75→        mock_instance = MagicMock()
    76→        mock_instance.with_structured_output.return_value = mock_chain
    77→        mock_chat_cls.return_value = mock_instance
    78→
    79→        entity = _make_entity(
    80→            entity_text="HbA1c",
    81→            selected_code="C0011847",
    82→            selected_system="umls",
    83→            preferred_term="Hemoglobin A1c",
    84→            omop_concept_id="201826",
    85→        )
    86→
    87→        mappings = await generate_field_mappings(entity, "HbA1c < 7%")
    88→
    89→        assert len(mappings) == 1
    90→        assert mappings[0]["entity_concept_id"] == "C0011847"
    91→        assert mappings[0]["entity_concept_system"] == "umls"
    92→        assert mappings[0]["omop_concept_id"] == "201826"
    93→
    94→    @patch.dict(os.environ, {"GOOGLE_API_KEY": "test-key"})
    95→    @patch("langchain_google_genai.ChatGoogleGenerativeAI")
    96→    async def test_mappings_with_null_omop(self, mock_chat_cls: MagicMock) -> None:
    97→        """Missing omop_concept_id yields None in mapping."""

   159→
   160→class TestCriterionIdLookup:
   161→    """Test criterion_id-based direct lookup in persist."""
   162→
   163→    def test_direct_lookup_by_criterion_id(self) -> None:
   164→        """When criterion_id is provided, session.get() is used."""
   165→        from protocol_processor.nodes.persist import (

   176→            entity_text="diabetes",
   177→            field_mappings=None,
   178→            criterion_id="crit-direct",
   179→        )
   180→
   181→        assert result == "crit-direct"
   182→        mock_session.get.assert_called_once()
   183→        # Should NOT have done a substring search
   184→        mock_session.exec.assert_not_called()
   185→
   186→    @patch("sqlmodel.select")

   214→    def test_fallback_to_substring_when_criterion_id_not_found(
   215→        self, mock_select: MagicMock
   216→    ) -> None:
   217→        """session.get returns None -> falls back to substring search."""
   218→        from protocol_processor.nodes.persist import (
   219→            _find_criterion_and_update_mappings,
   220→        )
   221→
   222→        mock_session = MagicMock()
   223→        # Direct lookup returns None
   224→        mock_session.get.return_value = None
   225→        # Substring fallback finds a match
   226→        mock_criterion = _make_criteria_mock(id="crit-substr")
   227→        mock_session.exec.return_value.all.return_value = [mock_criterion]
   228→
   229→        result = _find_criterion_and_update_mappings(
   230→            session=mock_session,
   231→            batch_id="batch-001",
   232→            entity_text="diabetes",
   233→            field_mappings=None,
   234→            criterion_id="crit-nonexistent",
   235→        )
   236→
   237→        assert result == "crit-substr"
   238→        # Should have tried direct lookup first
   239→        mock_session.get.assert_called_once()
   240→        # Then fallen back to substring search
   241→        mock_session.exec.assert_called_once()
   242→

   244→# ===========================================================================
   245→# TestFieldMappingAccumulation
   246→# ===========================================================================
   247→
   248→
   249→class TestFieldMappingAccumulation:
   250→    """Test that field_mappings accumulate, not overwrite (Gap 2 fix)."""
   251→
   252→    def test_new_mappings_extend_existing(self) -> None:
   253→        """New field_mappings should be appended to existing ones."""
   254→        from protocol_processor.nodes.persist import (
   255→            _find_criterion_and_update_mappings,
   256→        )
   257→
   258→        existing_mappings = [
   259→            {
   260→                "entity": "HbA1c",
   261→                "relation": "<",
   262→                "value": "7",
   263→                "unit": "%",
   264→            }
   265→        ]
   266→        mock_criterion = _make_criteria_mock(
   267→            id="crit-accum",
   268→            conditions={"field_mappings": existing_mappings},
   269→        )
   270→        mock_session = MagicMock()
   271→        mock_session.get.return_value = mock_criterion
   272→
   273→        new_mappings = [
   274→            {
   275→                "entity": "eGFR",
   276→                "relation": ">",
   277→                "value": "30",
   278→                "unit": "mL/min",
   279→            }
   280→        ]
   281→
   282→        _find_criterion_and_update_mappings(
   283→            session=mock_session,
   284→            batch_id="batch-001",
   285→            entity_text="eGFR",
   286→            field_mappings=new_mappings,
   287→            criterion_id="crit-accum",
   288→        )
   289→
   290→        final = mock_criterion.conditions["field_mappings"]
   291→        assert len(final) == 2
   292→        entities = [m["entity"] for m in final]
   293→        assert "HbA1c" in entities
   294→        assert "eGFR" in entities
   295→
   296→    def test_first_entity_creates_list(self) -> None:
   297→        """Empty conditions gets field_mappings set directly."""
   298→        from protocol_processor.nodes.persist import (
   299→            _find_criterion_and_update_mappings,
   300→        )
   301→
   302→        mock_criterion = _make_criteria_mock(id="crit-new", conditions={})
   303→        mock_session = MagicMock()
   304→        mock_session.get.return_value = mock_criterion
   305→
   306→        new_mappings = [
   307→            {
   308→                "entity": "HbA1c",
   309→                "relation": "<",
   310→                "value": "7",
   311→                "unit": "%",
   312→            }
   313→        ]
   314→
   315→        _find_criterion_and_update_mappings(
   316→            session=mock_session,
   317→            batch_id="batch-001",
   318→            entity_text="HbA1c",
   319→            field_mappings=new_mappings,

   322→
   323→        final = mock_criterion.conditions["field_mappings"]
   324→        assert len(final) == 1
   325→        assert final[0]["entity"] == "HbA1c"
   326→
   327→    def test_multiple_calls_accumulate(self) -> None:
   328→        """Three calls should accumulate all mappings."""
   329→        from protocol_processor.nodes.persist import (
   330→            _find_criterion_and_update_mappings,
   331→        )
   332→
   333→        mock_criterion = _make_criteria_mock(id="crit-multi", conditions={})
   334→        mock_session = MagicMock()
   335→        mock_session.get.return_value = mock_criterion
   336→
   337→        entities_and_mappings = [
   338→            (
   339→                "HbA1c",
   340→                [{"entity": "HbA1c", "relation": "<", "value": "7", "unit": "%"}],
   341→            ),
   342→            (
   343→                "eGFR",
   344→                [{"entity": "eGFR", "relation": ">", "value": "30", "unit": "mL/min"}],
   345→            ),
   346→            (
   347→                "BMI",
   348→                [{"entity": "BMI", "relation": "<", "value": "40", "unit": "kg/m2"}],
   349→            ),
   350→        ]
   351→
   352→        for entity_text, mappings in entities_and_mappings:
   353→            _find_criterion_and_update_mappings(
   354→                session=mock_session,
   355→                batch_id="batch-001",
   356→                entity_text=entity_text,
   357→                field_mappings=mappings,
   358→                criterion_id="crit-multi",
   359→            )
   360→
   361→        final = mock_criterion.conditions["field_mappings"]
   362→        assert len(final) == 3
   363→        entities = [m["entity"] for m in final]
   364→        assert entities == ["HbA1c", "eGFR", "BMI"]

   367→# ===========================================================================
   368→# TestEntityGroundingResultHasCriterionId
   369→# ===========================================================================

   371→
   372→class TestEntityGroundingResultHasCriterionId:
   373→    """Schema tests for Phase 1b fields on EntityGroundingResult."""
   374→
   375→    def test_criterion_id_field_exists(self) -> None:
   376→        """criterion_id round-trips through model_dump."""
   377→        entity = _make_entity(criterion_id="test-id")
   378→        dumped = entity.model_dump()
   379→
   380→        assert dumped["criterion_id"] == "test-id"
   381→        # Round-trip: reconstruct from dump
   382→        reconstructed = EntityGroundingResult(**dumped)
   383→        assert reconstructed.criterion_id == "test-id"
   384→
   385→    def test_omop_fields_exist(self) -> None:
   386→        """omop_concept_id and reconciliation_status are accepted."""
   387→        entity = _make_entity(
   388→            omop_concept_id="201826",
   389→            reconciliation_status="agree",
   390→        )
   391→        dumped = entity.model_dump()
   392→
   393→        assert dumped["omop_concept_id"] == "201826"
   394→        assert dumped["reconciliation_status"] == "agree"
   395→
   396→    def test_criterion_id_defaults_to_none(self) -> None:
   397→        """criterion_id should default to None when not provided."""
   398→        entity = _make_entity()
   399→        assert entity.criterion_id is None
   400→
   401→    def test_omop_fields_default_to_none(self) -> None:
   402→        """OMOP and reconciliation fields default to None."""
   403→        entity = _make_entity()
   404→        assert entity.omop_concept_id is None
   405→        assert entity.reconciliation_status is None
   406→

The `frontend` job passes on main — so this is caused by my changes. The issue is that my test imports `CriterionCard.tsx`, which transitively imports many untested components (`CriterionModifyDialog`, `CriterionRerunPanel`, `FieldMappingBadges`, etc.), dragging down overall coverage from the threshold.

The fix: extract the pure utility functions into a separate file so the test doesn't pull in the entire component tree.

   161→        '<=': '<=',
   162→        '>': '>',
   163→        '<': '<',
   164→        '==': '=',
   165→        '=': '=',
   166→        range: 'within',
   167→    };
   168→    return map[comparator] ?? '';
   169→}
   170→
   171→/** Parse a duration string like "6 months" into {value, unit}. */
   172→function parseDuration(duration: string): {
   173→    value: string;
   174→    unit: TemporalUnit;
   175→} {
   176→    const match = duration.match(/^(\d+)\s*(days?|weeks?|months?|years?)$/i);
   177→    if (match) {
   178→        const raw = match[2].toLowerCase().replace(/s$/, '');
   179→        const unitMap: Record<string, TemporalUnit> = {
   180→            day: 'days',
   181→            week: 'weeks',
   182→            month: 'months',
   183→            year: 'years',
   184→        };
   185→        return { value: match[1], unit: unitMap[raw] ?? 'days' };
   186→    }
   187→    return { value: duration, unit: 'days' };
   188→}
   189→
   190→/** Valid frontend relation operators. */
   191→const VALID_RELATIONS = new Set<string>([
   192→    '=',
   193→    '!=',
   194→    '>',
   195→    '>=',
   196→    '<',
   197→    '<=',
   198→    'within',
   199→    'not_in_last',
   200→    'contains',
   201→    'not_contains',
   202→]);
   203→
   204→/** Normalize a raw relation string to a valid RelationOperator or empty. */
   205→export function normalizeRelation(raw: string): RelationOperator | '' {
   206→    const map: Record<string, RelationOperator> = {
   207→        has: 'contains',
   208→        is: '=',
   209→        not: 'not_contains',
   210→        '==': '=',
   211→        range: 'within',
   212→    };
   213→    const mapped = map[raw] ?? raw;
   214→    return VALID_RELATIONS.has(mapped) ? (mapped as RelationOperator) : '';
   215→}
   216→
   217→/** Normalize a field mapping's value to a typed FieldValue. */
   218→export function normalizeFieldValue(fm: Record<string, unknown>): FieldValue {
   219→    const rawVal = fm.value;
   220→
   221→    // New typed format: value is an object with a 'type' key
   222→    if (rawVal && typeof rawVal === 'object' && !Array.isArray(rawVal)) {
   223→        const obj = rawVal as Record<string, unknown>;
   224→        if (obj.type === 'range') {
   225→            return {
   226→                type: 'range',
   227→                min: String(obj.min ?? ''),
   228→                max: String(obj.max ?? ''),
   229→                unit: String(obj.unit ?? ''),
   230→            };
   231→        }
   232→        if (obj.type === 'temporal') {
   233→            return {
   234→                type: 'temporal',
   235→                duration: String(obj.duration ?? ''),
   236→                unit: (obj.unit as TemporalUnit) ?? 'days',
   237→            };
   238→        }
   239→        if (obj.type === 'standard') {
   240→            return {
   241→                type: 'standard',
   242→                value: String(obj.value ?? ''),
   243→                unit: String(obj.unit ?? ''),
   244→            };
   245→        }
   246→    }
   247→
   248→    // Legacy flat format: value is a string or number
   249→    if (typeof rawVal === 'string' || typeof rawVal === 'number') {
   250→        return {
   251→            type: 'standard',
   252→            value: String(rawVal),
   253→            unit: String(fm.unit ?? ''),
   254→        };
   255→    }
   256→
   257→    return { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' };
   258→}
   259→
   260→/**
   261→ * Build initial form values for the structured editor from a criterion's
   262→ * AI-extracted data (numeric_thresholds, temporal_constraint, conditions).
   263→ */
   264→export function buildInitialValues(criterion: Criterion): StructuredFieldFormValues {
   265→    // Priority 1: existing field_mappings from a previous structured edit
   266→    const cond = criterion.conditions as Record<string, unknown> | null;
   267→    if (cond && 'field_mappings' in cond && Array.isArray(cond.field_mappings)) {
   268→        const fms = cond.field_mappings as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
   269→        const mappings: FieldMapping[] = fms.map((fm) => {
   270→            const rel = (fm.relation as string) ?? '';
   271→            const value = normalizeFieldValue(fm);
   272→            return {
   273→                entity: String(fm.entity ?? ''),
   274→                entity_code:
   275→                    (fm.entity_code ?? fm.entity_concept_id)
   276→                        ? String(fm.entity_code ?? fm.entity_concept_id)
   277→                        : undefined,
   278→                entity_system:
   279→                    (fm.entity_system ?? fm.entity_concept_system)
   280→                        ? String(fm.entity_system ?? fm.entity_concept_system)
   281→                        : undefined,
   282→                omop_concept_id: fm.omop_concept_id ? String(fm.omop_concept_id) : undefined,
   283→                relation: normalizeRelation(rel),
   284→                value,

   264→export function buildInitialValues(criterion: Criterion): StructuredFieldFormValues {
   265→    // Priority 1: existing field_mappings from a previous structured edit
   266→    const cond = criterion.conditions as Record<string, unknown> | null;
   267→    if (cond && 'field_mappings' in cond && Array.isArray(cond.field_mappings)) {

   274→                entity_code:
   275→                    (fm.entity_code ?? fm.entity_concept_id)
   276→                        ? String(fm.entity_code ?? fm.entity_concept_id)
   277→                        : undefined,
   278→                entity_system:
   279→                    (fm.entity_system ?? fm.entity_concept_system)
   280→                        ? String(fm.entity_system ?? fm.entity_concept_system)
   281→                        : undefined,
   282→                omop_concept_id: fm.omop_concept_id ? String(fm.omop_concept_id) : undefined,
   283→                relation: normalizeRelation(rel),
   284→                value,
   285→            };
   286→        });
   287→        if (mappings.length > 0) return { mappings };
   288→    }

   291→    const mappings: FieldMapping[] = [];
   292→
   293→    const entities = criterion.entities ?? [];
   294→    const thresholds = extractThresholdsList(criterion.numeric_thresholds);
   295→
   296→    const entityLabel = (e: { preferred_term: string | null; text: string }) =>
   297→        e.preferred_term || e.text;
   298→
   299→    const measurableEntities = entities.filter(
   300→        (e) =>
   301→            e.entity_type === 'Lab_Value' ||

   306→    const usedEntityIndices = new Set<number>();

   308→    for (let i = 0; i < thresholds.length; i++) {
   309→        const t = thresholds[i];
   310→        const comparator = (t.comparator as string) ?? '';
   311→        const val = t.value as number | null;
   312→        const unit = (t.unit as string) ?? '';
   313→        const upperVal = t.upper_value as number | null;
   314→
   315→        if (val === null || val === undefined) continue;

   317→        // Find the next unused measurable entity

   321→                matchedEntity = measurableEntities[j];
   322→                usedEntityIndices.add(j);
   323→                break;
   324→            }
   325→        }

   329→            ? 'snomed'
   330→            : matchedEntity?.umls_cui
   331→              ? 'umls'
   332→              : undefined;
   333→
   334→        if (comparator === 'range' && upperVal != null) {

   337→                entity_code: entityCode,
   338→                entity_system: entitySystem,
   339→                relation: 'within' as RelationOperator,

   341→            });
   342→        } else {
   343→            const relation = mapComparator(comparator);
   344→            mappings.push({
   345→                entity,
   346→                entity_code: entityCode,
   347→                entity_system: entitySystem,
   348→                relation,
   349→                value: { type: 'standard', value: String(val), unit },
   350→            });
   351→        }
   352→    }
   353→
   354→    // Add remaining unmatched measurable entities with empty relation/value

'days',\n            };\n        }\n        if (obj.type === 'standard') {\n            return {\n                type: 'standard',\n                value: String(obj.value ?? ''),\n                unit: String(obj.unit ?? ''),\n            };\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Legacy flat format: value is a string or number\n    if (typeof rawVal === 'string' || typeof rawVal === 'number') {\n        return {\n            type: 'standard',\n            value: String(rawVal),\n            unit: String(fm.unit ?? ''),\n        };\n    }\n\n    return { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' };\n}\n\n/**\n * Build initial form values for the structured editor from a criterion's\n * AI-extracted data (numeric_thresholds, temporal_constraint, conditions).\n */\nexport function buildInitialValues(criterion: Criterion): StructuredFieldFormValues {\n    // Priority 1: existing field_mappings from a previous structured edit\n    const cond = criterion.conditions as Record<string, unknown> | null;\n    if (cond && 'field_mappings' in cond && Array.isArray(cond.field_mappings)) {\n        const fms = cond.field_mappings as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;\n        const mappings: FieldMapping[] = fms.map((fm) => {\n            const rel = (fm.relation as string) ?? '';\n            const value = normalizeFieldValue(fm);\n            return {\n                entity: String(fm.entity ?? ''),\n                entity_code:\n                    (fm.entity_code ?? fm.entity_concept_id)\n                        ? String(fm.entity_code ?? fm.entity_concept_id)\n                        : undefined,\n                entity_system:\n                    (fm.entity_system ?? fm.entity_concept_system)\n                        ? String(fm.entity_system ?? fm.entity_concept_system)\n                        : undefined,\n                omop_concept_id: fm.omop_concept_id ? String(fm.omop_concept_id) : undefined,\n                relation: normalizeRelation(rel),\n                value,\n            };\n        });\n        if (mappings.length > 0) return { mappings };\n    }\n\n    // Priority 2: infer from AI-extracted data (entities + thresholds + temporal)\n    const mappings: FieldMapping[] = [];\n\n    const entities = criterion.entities ?? [];\n    const thresholds = extractThresholdsList(criterion.numeric_thresholds);\n\n    const entityLabel = (e: { preferred_term: string | null; text: string }) =>\n        e.preferred_term || e.text;\n\n    const measurableEntities = entities.filter(\n        (e) =>\n            e.entity_type === 'Lab_Value' ||\n            e.entity_type === 'Biomarker' ||\n            e.entity_type === 'Demographic'\n    );\n\n    const usedEntityIndices = new Set<number>();\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < thresholds.length; i++) {\n        const t = thresholds[i];\n        const comparator = (t.comparator as string) ?? '';\n        const val = t.value as number | null;\n        const unit = (t.unit as string) ?? '';\n        const upperVal = t.upper_value as number | null;\n\n        if (val === null || val === undefined) continue;\n\n        // Find the next unused measurable entity\n        let matchedEntity: (typeof measurableEntities)[number] | undefined;\n        for (let j = 0; j < measurableEntities.length; j++) {\n            if (!usedEntityIndices.has(j)) {\n                matchedEntity = measurableEntities[j];\n                usedEntityIndices.add(j);\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        const entity = matchedEntity ? entityLabel(matchedEntity) : '';\n        const entityCode = matchedEntity?.snomed_code ?? matchedEntity?.umls_cui ?? undefined;\n        const entitySystem = matchedEntity?.snomed_code\n            ? 'snomed'\n            : matchedEntity?.umls_cui\n              ? 'umls'\n              : undefined;\n\n        if (comparator === 'range' && upperVal != null) {\n            mappings.push({\n                entity,\n                entity_code: entityCode,\n                entity_system: entitySystem,\n                relation: 'within' as RelationOperator,\n                value: { type: 'range', min: String(val), max: String(upperVal), unit },\n            });\n        } else {\n            const relation = mapComparator(comparator);\n            mappings.push({\n                entity,\n                entity_code: entityCode,\n                entity_system: entitySystem,\n                relation,\n                value: { type: 'standard', value: String(val), unit },\n            });\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Add remaining unmatched measurable entities with empty relation/value\n    for (let j = 0; j < measurableEntities.length; j++) {\n        if (!usedEntityIndices.has(j)) {\n            const e = measurableEntities[j];\n            const code = e.snomed_code ?? e.umls_cui ?? undefined;\n            const system = e.snomed_code ? 'snomed' : e.umls_cui ? 'umls' : undefined;\n            mappings.push({\n                entity: entityLabel(e),\n                entity_code: code,\n                entity_system: system,\n                relation: '',\n                value: { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' },\n            });\n        }\n    }\n\n    const unmatchedEntities = entities.filter(\n        (e) =>\n            e.entity_type === 'Condition' ||\n            e.entity_type === 'Medication' ||\n            e.entity_type === 'Procedure'\n    );\n    for (const e of unmatchedEntities) {\n        const code = e.snomed_code ?? e.umls_cui ?? undefined;\n        const system = e.snomed_code ? 'snomed' : e.umls_cui ? 'umls' : undefined;\n        mappings.push({\n            entity: entityLabel(e),\n            entity_code: code,\n            entity_system: system,\n            relation: '',\n            value: { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' },\n        });\n    }\n\n    if (criterion.temporal_constraint) {\n        const tc = criterion.temporal_constraint;\n        const duration = tc.duration as string | undefined;\n        const referencePoint = tc.reference_point as string | undefined;\n\n        if (duration) {\n            const parsed = parseDuration(duration);\n            mappings.push({\n                entity: referencePoint ?? '',\n                relation: 'not_in_last' as RelationOperator,\n                value: { type: 'temporal', duration: parsed.value, unit: parsed.unit },\n            });\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (mappings.length === 0) return DEFAULT_FIELD_VALUES;\n    return { mappings };\n}\n"
}
