```
    30→export function ConfidenceBadge({ confidence }: { confidence: number }) {
    31→    const percentage = Math.round(confidence * 100);
    32→
    33→    let label: string;
    34→    let colorClass: string;
    35→
    36→    if (confidence >= 0.85) {
    37→        label = 'High';
    38→        colorClass = 'bg-green-100 text-green-800';
    39→    } else if (confidence >= 0.7) {
    40→        label = 'Medium';
    41→        colorClass = 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800';
    42→    } else {
    43→        label = 'Low';
    44→        colorClass = 'bg-red-100 text-red-800';
    45→    }
    46→
    47→    return (
    48→        <span
    49→            className={cn(
    50→                'inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium',
    51→                colorClass
    52→            )}
    53→        >
    54→            {label} ({percentage}%)
    55→        </span>
    56→    );
    57→}
    58→
    59→export function CriterionReviewStatusBadge({ status }: { status: string | null }) {
    60→    const statusConfig: Record<string, { label: string; colorClass: string }> = {
    61→        approved: { label: 'Approved', colorClass: 'bg-green-100 text-green-800' },
    62→        rejected: { label: 'Rejected', colorClass: 'bg-red-100 text-red-800' },
    63→        modified: { label: 'Modified', colorClass: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800' },
    64→    };
    65→
    66→    const config = status ? statusConfig[status] : null;
    67→    const label = config?.label ?? 'Pending';
    68→    const colorClass = config?.colorClass ?? 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-800';
    69→
    70→    return (
    71→        <span
    72→            className={cn(
    73→                'inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium',
    74→                colorClass
    75→            )}
    76→        >
    77→            {label}
    78→        </span>
    79→    );
    80→}
    81→
    82→export function CriteriaTypeBadge({ type }: { type: string }) {
    83→    const colorClass =
    84→        type === 'inclusion' ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800' : 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-800';
    85→
    86→    return (
    87→        <span
    88→            className={cn(
    89→                'inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium capitalize',
    90→                colorClass
    91→            )}
    92→        >
    93→            {type}
    94→        </span>
    95→    );
    96→}
    97→
    98→export function formatTemporalConstraint(tc: Record<string, unknown>): string {
    99→    const duration = 'duration' in tc ? (tc.duration as string) : null;
   100→    const relation = 'relation' in tc ? (tc.relation as string) : null;
   101→    const referencePoint = 'reference_point' in tc ? (tc.reference_point as string) : null;
   102→
   103→    if (!duration) return '';
   104→
   105→    const relationMap: Record<string, string> = {
   106→        within: 'Within',
   107→        before: 'Before',
   108→        after: 'After',
   109→        at_least: 'At least',
   110→    };
   111→
   112→    const relationText = relation ? relationMap[relation] || relation : '';
   113→    const parts = [relationText, duration];
   114→    if (referencePoint) parts.push('of', referencePoint);
   115→
   116→    return parts.filter(Boolean).join(' ');
   117→}
   118→
   119→export function formatNumericThreshold(threshold: Record<string, unknown>): string {
   120→    const value = 'value' in threshold ? threshold.value : null;
   121→    const unit = 'unit' in threshold ? (threshold.unit as string) : '';
   122→    const comparator = 'comparator' in threshold ? (threshold.comparator as string) : '';
   123→    const upperValue = 'upper_value' in threshold ? threshold.upper_value : null;
   124→
   125→    if (value === null) return '';
   126→
   127→    if (comparator === 'range' && upperValue !== null) {
   128→        return `${value}-${upperValue} ${unit}`.trim();
   129→    }
   130→
   131→    return `${comparator}${value} ${unit}`.trim();
   132→}
   133→
   134→export function extractThresholdsList(
   135→    nt: Record<string, unknown> | null
   136→): Array<Record<string, unknown>> {
   137→    if (!nt) return [];
   138→
   139→    // Shape 1: {"thresholds": [...]} wrapper object
   140→    if ('thresholds' in nt && Array.isArray(nt.thresholds)) {
   141→        return nt.thresholds as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
   142→    }
   143→
   144→    // Shape 2: raw array stored directly
   145→    if (Array.isArray(nt)) {
   146→        return nt as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
   147→    }
   148→
   149→    // Shape 3: single threshold object without wrapper
   150→    if ('value' in nt && 'comparator' in nt) {
   151→        return [nt];
   152→    }
   153→
   154→    return [];
   155→}
   156→
   157→/** Map extraction comparator strings to RelationOperator. */
   158→function mapComparator(comparator: string): RelationOperator | '' {
   159→    const map: Record<string, RelationOperator> = {
   160→        '>=': '>=',
   161→        '<=': '<=',
   162→        '>': '>',
   163→        '<': '<',
   164→        '==': '=',
   165→        '=': '=',
   166→        range: 'within',
   167→    };
   168→    return map[comparator] ?? '';
   169→}
   170→
   171→/** Parse a duration string like "6 months" into {value, unit}. */
   172→function parseDuration(duration: string): {
   173→    value: string;
   174→    unit: TemporalUnit;
   175→} {
   176→    const match = duration.match(/^(\d+)\s*(days?|weeks?|months?|years?)$/i);
   177→    if (match) {
   178→        const raw = match[2].toLowerCase().replace(/s$/, '');
   179→        const unitMap: Record<string, TemporalUnit> = {
   180→            day: 'days',
   181→            week: 'weeks',
   182→            month: 'months',
   183→            year: 'years',
   184→        };
   185→        return { value: match[1], unit: unitMap[raw] ?? 'days' };
   186→    }
   187→    return { value: duration, unit: 'days' };
   188→}
   189→
   190→/** Valid frontend relation operators. */
   191→const VALID_RELATIONS = new Set<string>([
   192→    '=',
   193→    '!=',
   194→    '>',
   195→    '>=',
   196→    '<',
   197→    '<=',
   198→    'within',
   199→    'not_in_last',
   200→    'contains',
   201→    'not_contains',
   202→]);
   203→
   204→/** Normalize a raw relation string to a valid RelationOperator or empty. */
   205→export function normalizeRelation(raw: string): RelationOperator | '' {
   206→    const map: Record<string, RelationOperator> = {
   207→        has: 'contains',
   208→        is: '=',
   209→        not: 'not_contains',
   210→        '==': '=',
   211→        range: 'within',
   212→    };
   213→    const mapped = map[raw] ?? raw;
   214→    return VALID_RELATIONS.has(mapped) ? (mapped as RelationOperator) : '';
   215→}
   216→
   217→/** Normalize a field mapping's value to a typed FieldValue. */
   218→export function normalizeFieldValue(fm: Record<string, unknown>): FieldValue {
   219→    const rawVal = fm.value;
   220→
   221→    // New typed format: value is an object with a 'type' key
   222→    if (rawVal && typeof rawVal === 'object' && !Array.isArray(rawVal)) {
   223→        const obj = rawVal as Record<string, unknown>;
   224→        if (obj.type === 'range') {
   225→            return {
   226→                type: 'range',
   227→                min: String(obj.min ?? ''),
   228→                max: String(obj.max ?? ''),
   229→                unit: String(obj.unit ?? ''),
   230→            };
   231→        }
   232→        if (obj.type === 'temporal') {
   233→            return {
   234→                type: 'temporal',
   235→                duration: String(obj.duration ?? ''),
   236→                unit: (obj.unit as TemporalUnit) ?? 'days',
   237→            };
   238→        }
   239→        if (obj.type === 'standard') {
   240→            return {
   241→                type: 'standard',
   242→                value: String(obj.value ?? ''),
   243→                unit: String(obj.unit ?? ''),
   244→            };
   245→        }
   246→    }
   247→
   248→    // Legacy flat format: value is a string or number
   249→    if (typeof rawVal === 'string' || typeof rawVal === 'number') {
   250→        return {
   251→            type: 'standard',
   252→            value: String(rawVal),
   253→            unit: String(fm.unit ?? ''),
   254→        };
   255→    }
   256→
   257→    return { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' };
   258→}
   259→
   260→/**
   261→ * Build initial form values for the structured editor from a criterion's
   262→ * AI-extracted data (numeric_thresholds, temporal_constraint, conditions).
   263→ */
   264→export function buildInitialValues(criterion: Criterion): StructuredFieldFormValues {
   265→    // Priority 1: existing field_mappings from a previous structured edit
   266→    const cond = criterion.conditions as Record<string, unknown> | null;
   267→    if (cond && 'field_mappings' in cond && Array.isArray(cond.field_mappings)) {
   268→        const fms = cond.field_mappings as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
   269→        const mappings: FieldMapping[] = fms.map((fm) => {
   270→            const rel = (fm.relation as string) ?? '';
   271→            const value = normalizeFieldValue(fm);
   272→            return {
   273→                entity: String(fm.entity ?? ''),
   274→                entity_code:
   275→                    (fm.entity_code ?? fm.entity_concept_id)
   276→                        ? String(fm.entity_code ?? fm.entity_concept_id)
   277→                        : undefined,
   278→                entity_system:
   279→                    (fm.entity_system ?? fm.entity_concept_system)
   280→                        ? String(fm.entity_system ?? fm.entity_concept_system)
   281→                        : undefined,
   282→                omop_concept_id: fm.omop_concept_id ? String(fm.omop_concept_id) : undefined,
   283→                relation: normalizeRelation(rel),
   284→                value,
   285→            };
   286→        });
   287→        if (mappings.length > 0) return { mappings };
   288→    }
   289→
   290→    // Priority 2: infer from AI-extracted data (entities + thresholds + temporal)
   291→    const mappings: FieldMapping[] = [];
   292→
   293→    const entities = criterion.entities ?? [];
   294→    const thresholds = extractThresholdsList(criterion.numeric_thresholds);
   295→
   296→    const entityLabel = (e: { preferred_term: string | null; text: string }) =>
   297→        e.preferred_term || e.text;
   298→
   299→    const measurableEntities = entities.filter(
   300→        (e) =>
   301→            e.entity_type === 'Lab_Value' ||
   302→            e.entity_type === 'Biomarker' ||
   303→            e.entity_type === 'Demographic'
   304→    );
   305→
   306→    const usedEntityIndices = new Set<number>();
   307→
   308→    for (let i = 0; i < thresholds.length; i++) {
   309→        const t = thresholds[i];
   310→        const comparator = (t.comparator as string) ?? '';
   311→        const val = t.value as number | null;
   312→        const unit = (t.unit as string) ?? '';
   313→        const upperVal = t.upper_value as number | null;
   314→
   315→        if (val === null || val === undefined) continue;
   316→
   317→        // Find the next unused measurable entity
   318→        let matchedEntity: (typeof measurableEntities)[number] | undefined;
   319→        for (let j = 0; j < measurableEntities.length; j++) {
   320→            if (!usedEntityIndices.has(j)) {
   321→                matchedEntity = measurableEntities[j];
   322→                usedEntityIndices.add(j);
   323→                break;
   324→            }
   325→        }
   326→        const entity = matchedEntity ? entityLabel(matchedEntity) : '';
   327→        const entityCode = matchedEntity?.snomed_code ?? matchedEntity?.umls_cui ?? undefined;
   328→        const entitySystem = matchedEntity?.snomed_code
   329→            ? 'snomed'
   330→            : matchedEntity?.umls_cui
   331→              ? 'umls'
   332→              : undefined;
   333→
   334→        if (comparator === 'range' && upperVal != null) {
   335→            mappings.push({
   336→                entity,
   337→                entity_code: entityCode,
   338→                entity_system: entitySystem,
   339→                relation: 'within' as RelationOperator,
   340→                value: { type: 'range', min: String(val), max: String(upperVal), unit },
   341→            });
   342→        } else {
   343→            const relation = mapComparator(comparator);
   344→            mappings.push({
   345→                entity,
   346→                entity_code: entityCode,
   347→                entity_system: entitySystem,
   348→                relation,
   349→                value: { type: 'standard', value: String(val), unit },
   350→            });
   351→        }
   352→    }
   353→
   354→    // Add remaining unmatched measurable entities with empty relation/value
   355→    for (let j = 0; j < measurableEntities.length; j++) {
   356→        if (!usedEntityIndices.has(j)) {
   357→            const e = measurableEntities[j];
   358→            const code = e.snomed_code ?? e.umls_cui ?? undefined;
   359→            const system = e.snomed_code ? 'snomed' : e.umls_cui ? 'umls' : undefined;
   360→            mappings.push({
   361→                entity: entityLabel(e),
   362→                entity_code: code,
   363→                entity_system: system,
   364→                relation: '',
   365→                value: { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' },
   366→            });
   367→        }
   368→    }
   369→
   370→    const unmatchedEntities = entities.filter(
   371→        (e) =>
   372→            e.entity_type === 'Condition' ||
   373→            e.entity_type === 'Medication' ||
   374→            e.entity_type === 'Procedure'
   375→    );
   376→    for (const e of unmatchedEntities) {
   377→        const code = e.snomed_code ?? e.umls_cui ?? undefined;
   378→        const system = e.snomed_code ? 'snomed' : e.umls_cui ? 'umls' : undefined;
   379→        mappings.push({
   380→            entity: entityLabel(e),
   381→            entity_code: code,
   382→            entity_system: system,
   383→            relation: '',
   384→            value: { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' },
   385→        });
   386→    }
   387→
   388→    if (criterion.temporal_constraint) {
   389→        const tc = criterion.temporal_constraint;
   390→        const duration = tc.duration as string | undefined;
   391→        const referencePoint = tc.reference_point as string | undefined;
   392→
   393→        if (duration) {
   394→            const parsed = parseDuration(duration);
   395→            mappings.push({
   396→                entity: referencePoint ?? '',
   397→                relation: 'not_in_last' as RelationOperator,
   398→                value: { type: 'temporal', duration: parsed.value, unit: parsed.unit },
   399→            });
   400→        }
   401→    }
   402→
   403→    if (mappings.length === 0) return DEFAULT_FIELD_VALUES;
   404→    return { mappings };
   405→}
   406→
   407→export default function CriterionCard({
   408→    criterion,
   409→    onAction,
   410→    isSubmitting,
   411→    onCriterionClick,
   412→    isActive,
   413→    pdfUrl,
   414→}: CriterionCardProps) {
   415→    const [modifyDialogOpen, setModifyDialogOpen] = useState(false);
   416→    const [rejectDialogOpen, setRejectDialogOpen] = useState(false);
   417→
   418→    function handleApprove() {
   419→        onAction(criterion.id, {
   420→            action: 'approve',
   421→            reviewer_id: 'current-user',
   422→        });
   423→    }
   424→
   425→    function handleReject() {
   426→        setRejectDialogOpen(true);
   427→    }
   428→
   429→    function handleRejectConfirm(data: { reasons: string[]; comment?: string }) {
   430→        onAction(criterion.id, {
   431→            action: 'reject',
   432→            reviewer_id: 'current-user',
   433→            reject_reasons: data.reasons,
   434→            comment: data.comment,
   435→        });
   436→    }
   437→
   438→    return (
   439→        <div
   440→            className={cn(
   441→                'rounded-lg border bg-card p-4 shadow-sm border-l-4',
   442→                criterion.review_status === 'approved' && 'border-l-green-500',
   443→                criterion.review_status === 'rejected' && 'border-l-red-500',
   444→                criterion.review_status === 'modified' && 'border-l-blue-500',
   445→                !criterion.review_status && 'border-l-yellow-400'
   446→            )}
   447→        >
   448→            {/* Header row */}
   449→            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mb-3">
   450→                <CriteriaTypeBadge type={criterion.criteria_type} />
   451→                {criterion.category && (
   452→                    <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-gray-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">
   453→                        {criterion.category}
   454→                    </span>
   455→                )}
   456→                <ConfidenceBadge confidence={criterion.confidence} />
   457→                <CriterionReviewStatusBadge status={criterion.review_status} />
   458→                {criterion.page_number != null && (
   459→                    <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 ml-auto">
   460→                        p.{criterion.page_number}
   461→                    </span>
   462→                )}
   463→            </div>
   464→
   465→            {/* Body - criterion text (read-only, clickable for PDF navigation) */}
   466→            <p
   467→                className={cn(
   468→                    'text-sm text-foreground mb-3',
   469→                    criterion.page_number != null &&
   470→                        'cursor-pointer hover:bg-accent/50 rounded px-1 -mx-1 transition-colors',
   471→                    isActive && 'bg-accent/30 rounded px-1 -mx-1'
   472→                )}
   473→                onClick={() => {
   474→                    if (criterion.page_number != null && onCriterionClick) {
   475→                        onCriterionClick(criterion);
   476→                    }
   477→                }}
   478→                onKeyDown={(e) => {
   479→                    if (
   480→                        (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') &&
   481→                        criterion.page_number != null &&
   482→                        onCriterionClick
   483→                    ) {
   484→                        onCriterionClick(criterion);
   485→                    }
   486→                }}
   487→                role={criterion.page_number != null ? 'button' : undefined}
   488→                tabIndex={criterion.page_number != null ? 0 : undefined}
   489→                title={
   490→                    criterion.page_number != null
   491→                        ? `Click to view source (page ${criterion.page_number})`
   492→                        : undefined
   493→                }
   494→            >
   495→                {criterion.text}
   496→            </p>
   497→
   498→            {/* Field mapping badges */}
   499→            <FieldMappingBadges
   500→                criterion={criterion}
   501→                onEditClick={() => setModifyDialogOpen(true)}
   502→            />
   503→
   504→            {/* Assertion status tag */}
   505→            {criterion.assertion_status && criterion.assertion_status !== 'PRESENT' && (
   506→                <div className="mb-3">
   507→                    <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-purple-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-purple-800">
   508→                        {criterion.assertion_status}
   509→                    </span>
   510→                </div>
   511→            )}
   512→
   513→            {/* Temporal constraint */}
   514→            {criterion.temporal_constraint &&
   515→                formatTemporalConstraint(criterion.temporal_constraint) && (
   516→                    <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5">
   517→                        <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-indigo-600" />
   518→                        <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-indigo-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-indigo-800">
   519→                            {formatTemporalConstraint(criterion.temporal_constraint)}
   520→                        </span>
   521→                    </div>
   522→                )}
   523→
   524→            {/* Numeric thresholds */}
   525→            {extractThresholdsList(criterion.numeric_thresholds).length > 0 && (
   526→                <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
   527→                    <Hash className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-teal-600" />
   528→                    {extractThresholdsList(criterion.numeric_thresholds).map((threshold) => {
   529→                        const text = formatNumericThreshold(threshold);
   530→                        return text ? (
   531→                            <span
   532→                                key={text}
   533→                                className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-teal-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-teal-800"
   534→                            >
   535→                                {text}
   536→                            </span>
   537→                        ) : null;
   538→                    })}
   539→                </div>
   540→            )}
   541→
   542→            {/* Action buttons */}
   543→            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-2 border-t">
   544→                <Button
   545→                    size="sm"
   546→                    variant="outline"
   547→                    className="text-green-700 border-green-300 hover:bg-green-50"
   548→                    onClick={handleApprove}
   549→                    disabled={isSubmitting || criterion.review_status === 'approved'}
   550→                >
   551→                    {isSubmitting ? (
   552→                        <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin mr-1" />
   553→                    ) : (
   554→                        <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
   555→                    )}
   556→                    Approve
   557→                </Button>
   558→                <Button
   559→                    size="sm"
   560→                    variant="outline"
   561→                    className="text-red-700 border-red-300 hover:bg-red-50"
   562→                    onClick={handleReject}
   563→                    disabled={isSubmitting || criterion.review_status === 'rejected'}
   564→                >
   565→                    {isSubmitting ? (
   566→                        <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin mr-1" />
   567→                    ) : (
   568→                        <XCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
   569→                    )}
   570→                    Reject
   571→                </Button>
   572→                <Button
   573→                    size="sm"
   574→                    variant="outline"
   575→                    className="text-blue-700 border-blue-300 hover:bg-blue-50"
   576→                    onClick={() => setModifyDialogOpen(true)}
   577→                    disabled={isSubmitting}
   578→                >
   579→                    <Pencil className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
   580→                    Modify
   581→                </Button>
   582→                <CriterionRerunPanel
   583→                    criterionId={criterion.id}
   584→                    criterionText={criterion.text}
   585→                    currentExtraction={{
   586→                        criteria_type: criterion.criteria_type,
   587→                        category: criterion.category,
   588→                        temporal_constraint: criterion.temporal_constraint,
   589→                        conditions: criterion.conditions,
   590→                        numeric_thresholds: criterion.numeric_thresholds,
   591→                        text: criterion.text,
   592→                    }}
   593→                    onAccept={(revised) => {
   594→                        onAction(criterion.id, {
   595→                            action: 'modify',
   596→                            reviewer_id: 'current-user',
   597→                            modified_text: revised.text as string | undefined,
   598→                            modified_type: revised.criteria_type as string | undefined,
   599→                            modified_category: revised.category as string | undefined,
   600→                            modified_structured_fields: revised,
   601→                            comment: 'AI-assisted correction with reviewer feedback',
   602→                        });
   603→                    }}
   604→                    trigger={
   605→                        <Button
   606→                            size="sm"
   607→                            variant="outline"
   608→                            className="text-purple-700 border-purple-300 hover:bg-purple-50"
   609→                            disabled={isSubmitting}
   610→                        >
   611→                            <Wand2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
   612→                            Correct with AI
   613→                        </Button>
   614→                    }
   615→                />
   616→            </div>
   617→
   618→            {/* Audit history section */}
   619→            <CriterionAuditHistory criterionId={criterion.id} />
   620→
   621→            <RejectDialog
   622→                open={rejectDialogOpen}
   623→                onOpenChange={setRejectDialogOpen}
   624→                onConfirm={handleRejectConfirm}
   625→            />
   626→
   627→            <CriterionModifyDialog
   628→                open={modifyDialogOpen}
   629→                onOpenChange={setModifyDialogOpen}
   630→                criterion={criterion}
   631→                onAction={(action) => onAction(criterion.id, action)}
   632→                isSubmitting={isSubmitting}
   633→                pdfUrl={pdfUrl}
   634→            />
   635→        </div>
   636→    );
   637→}
   638→
```

```json
  "old_string": "export function formatTemporalConstraint(tc: Record<string, unknown>): string {\n    const duration = 'duration' in tc ? (tc.duration as string) : null;\n    const relation = 'relation' in tc ? (tc.relation as string) : null;\n    const referencePoint = 'reference_point' in tc ? (tc.reference_point as string) : null;\n\n    if (!duration) return '';\n\n    const relationMap: Record<string, string> = {\n        within: 'Within',\n        before: 'Before',\n        after: 'After',\n        at_least: 'At least',\n    };\n\n    const relationText = relation ? relationMap[relation] || relation : '';\n    const parts = [relationText, duration];\n    if (referencePoint) parts.push('of', referencePoint);\n\n    return parts.filter(Boolean).join(' ');\n}\n\nexport function formatNumericThreshold(threshold: Record<string, unknown>): string {\n    const value = 'value' in threshold ? threshold.value : null;\n    const unit = 'unit' in threshold ? (threshold.unit as string) : '';\n    const comparator = 'comparator' in threshold ? (threshold.comparator as string) : '';\n    const upperValue = 'upper_value' in threshold ? threshold.upper_value : null;\n\n    if (value === null) return '';\n\n    if (comparator === 'range' && upperValue !== null) {\n        return `${value}-${upperValue} ${unit}`.trim();\n    }\n\n    return `${comparator}${value} ${unit}`.trim();\n}\n\nexport function extractThresholdsList(\n    nt: Record<string, unknown> | null\n): Array<Record<string, unknown>> {\n    if (!nt) return [];\n\n    // Shape 1: {\"thresholds\": [...]} wrapper object\n    if ('thresholds' in nt && Array.isArray(nt.thresholds)) {\n        return nt.thresholds as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;\n    }\n\n    // Shape 2: raw array stored directly\n    if (Array.isArray(nt)) {\n        return nt as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;\n    }\n\n    // Shape 3: single threshold object without wrapper\n    if ('value' in nt && 'comparator' in nt) {\n        return [nt];\n    }\n\n    return [];\n}\n\n/** Map extraction comparator strings to RelationOperator. */\nfunction mapComparator(comparator: string): RelationOperator | '' {\n    const map: Record<string, RelationOperator> = {\n        '>=': '>=',\n        '<=': '<=',\n        '>': '>',\n        '<': '<',\n        '==': '=',\n        '=': '=',\n        range: 'within',\n    };\n    return map[comparator] ?? '';\n}\n\n/** Parse a duration string like \"6 months\" into {value, unit}. */\nfunction parseDuration(duration: string): {\n    value: string;\n    unit: TemporalUnit;\n} {\n    const match = duration.match(/^(\\d+)\\s*(days?|weeks?|months?|years?)$/i);\n    if (match) {\n        const raw = match[2].toLowerCase().replace(/s$/, '');\n        const unitMap: Record<string, TemporalUnit> = {\n            day: 'days',\n            week: 'weeks',\n            month: 'months',\n            year: 'years',\n        };\n        return { value: match[1], unit: unitMap[raw] ?? 'days' };\n    }\n    return { value: duration, unit: 'days' };\n}\n\n/** Valid frontend relation operators. */\nconst VALID_RELATIONS = new Set<string>([\n    '=',\n    '!=',\n    '>',\n    '>=',\n    '<',\n    '<=',\n    'within',\n    'not_in_last',\n    'contains',\n    'not_contains',\n]);\n\n/** Normalize a raw relation string to a valid RelationOperator or empty. */\nexport function normalizeRelation(raw: string): RelationOperator | '' {\n    const map: Record<string, RelationOperator> = {\n        has: 'contains',\n        is: '=',\n        not: 'not_contains',\n        '==': '=',\n        range: 'within',\n    };\n    const mapped = map[raw] ?? raw;\n    return VALID_RELATIONS.has(mapped) ? (mapped as RelationOperator) : '';\n}\n\n/** Normalize a field mapping's value to a typed FieldValue. */\nexport function normalizeFieldValue(fm: Record<string, unknown>): FieldValue {\n    const rawVal = fm.value;\n\n    // New typed format: value is an object with a 'type' key\n    if (rawVal && typeof rawVal === 'object' && !Array.isArray(rawVal)) {\n        const obj = rawVal as Record<string, unknown>;\n        if (obj.type === 'range') {\n            return {\n                type: 'range',\n                min: String(obj.min ?? ''),\n                max: String(obj.max ?? ''),\n                unit: String(obj.unit ?? ''),\n            };\n        }\n        if (obj.type === 'temporal') {\n            return {\n                type: 'temporal',\n                duration: String(obj.duration ?? ''),\n                unit: (obj.unit as TemporalUnit) ?? 'days',\n            };\n        }\n        if (obj.type === 'standard') {\n            return {\n                type: 'standard',\n                value: String(obj.value ?? ''),\n                unit: String(obj.unit ?? ''),\n            };\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Legacy flat format: value is a string or number\n    if (typeof rawVal === 'string' || typeof rawVal === 'number') {\n        return {\n            type: 'standard',\n            value: String(rawVal),\n            unit: String(fm.unit ?? ''),\n        };\n    }\n\n    return { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' };\n}\n\n/**\n * Build initial form values for the structured editor from a criterion's\n * AI-extracted data (numeric_thresholds, temporal_constraint, conditions).\n */\nexport function buildInitialValues(criterion: Criterion): StructuredFieldFormValues {\n    // Priority 1: existing field_mappings from a previous structured edit\n    const cond = criterion.conditions as Record<string, unknown> | null;\n    if (cond && 'field_mappings' in cond && Array.isArray(cond.field_mappings)) {\n        const fms = cond.field_mappings as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;\n        const mappings: FieldMapping[] = fms.map((fm) => {\n            const rel = (fm.relation as string) ?? '';\n            const value = normalizeFieldValue(fm);\n            return {\n                entity: String(fm.entity ?? ''),\n                entity_code:\n                    (fm.entity_code ?? fm.entity_concept_id)\n                        ? String(fm.entity_code ?? fm.entity_concept_id)\n                        : undefined,\n                entity_system:\n                    (fm.entity_system ?? fm.entity_concept_system)\n                        ? String(fm.entity_system ?? fm.entity_concept_system)\n                        : undefined,\n                omop_concept_id: fm.omop_concept_id ? String(fm.omop_concept_id) : undefined,\n                relation: normalizeRelation(rel),\n                value,\n            };\n        });\n        if (mappings.length > 0) return { mappings };\n    }\n\n    // Priority 2: infer from AI-extracted data (entities + thresholds + temporal)\n    const mappings: FieldMapping[] = [];\n\n    const entities = criterion.entities ?? [];\n    const thresholds = extractThresholdsList(criterion.numeric_thresholds);\n\n    const entityLabel = (e: { preferred_term: string | null; text: string }) =>\n        e.preferred_term || e.text;\n\n    const measurableEntities = entities.filter(\n        (e) =>\n            e.entity_type === 'Lab_Value' ||\n            e.entity_type === 'Biomarker' ||\n            e.entity_type === 'Demographic'\n    );\n\n    const usedEntityIndices = new Set<number>();\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < thresholds.length; i++) {\n        const t = thresholds[i];\n        const comparator = (t.comparator as string) ?? '';\n        const val = t.value as number | null;\n        const unit = (t.unit as string) ?? '';\n        const upperVal = t.upper_value as number | null;\n\n        if (val === null || val === undefined) continue;\n\n        // Find the next unused measurable entity\n        let matchedEntity: (typeof measurableEntities)[number] | undefined;\n        for (let j = 0; j < measurableEntities.length; j++) {\n            if (!usedEntityIndices.has(j)) {\n                matchedEntity = measurableEntities[j];\n                usedEntityIndices.add(j);\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        const entity = matchedEntity ? entityLabel(matchedEntity) : '';\n        const entityCode = matchedEntity?.snomed_code ?? matchedEntity?.umls_cui ?? undefined;\n        const entitySystem = matchedEntity?.snomed_code\n            ? 'snomed'\n            : matchedEntity?.umls_cui\n              ? 'umls'\n              : undefined;\n\n        if (comparator === 'range' && upperVal != null) {\n            mappings.push({\n                entity,\n                entity_code: entityCode,\n                entity_system: entitySystem,\n                relation: 'within' as RelationOperator,\n                value: { type: 'range', min: String(val), max: String(upperVal), unit },\n            });\n        } else {\n            const relation = mapComparator(comparator);\n            mappings.push({\n                entity,\n                entity_code: entityCode,\n                entity_system: entitySystem,\n                relation,\n                value: { type: 'standard', value: String(val), unit },\n            });\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Add remaining unmatched measurable entities with empty relation/value\n    for (let j = 0; j < measurableEntities.length; j++) {\n        if (!usedEntityIndices.has(j)) {\n            const e = measurableEntities[j];\n            const code = e.snomed_code ?? e.umls_cui ?? undefined;\n            const system = e.snomed_code ? 'snomed' : e.umls_cui ? 'umls' : undefined;\n            mappings.push({\n                entity: entityLabel(e),\n                entity_code: code,\n                entity_system: system,\n                relation: '',\n                value: { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' },\n            });\n        }\n    }\n\n    const unmatchedEntities = entities.filter(\n        (e) =>\n            e.entity_type === 'Condition' ||\n            e.entity_type === 'Medication' ||\n            e.entity_type === 'Procedure'\n    );\n    for (const e of unmatchedEntities) {\n        const code = e.snomed_code ?? e.umls_cui ?? undefined;\n        const system = e.snomed_code ? 'snomed' : e.umls_cui ? 'umls' : undefined;\n        mappings.push({\n            entity: entityLabel(e),\n            entity_code: code,\n            entity_system: system,\n            relation: '',\n            value: { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' },\n        });\n    }\n\n    if (criterion.temporal_constraint) {\n        const tc = criterion.temporal_constraint;\n        const duration = tc.duration as string | undefined;\n        const referencePoint = tc.reference_point as string | undefined;\n\n        if (duration) {\n            const parsed = parseDuration(duration);\n            mappings.push({\n                entity: referencePoint ?? '',\n                relation: 'not_in_last' as RelationOperator,\n                value: { type: 'temporal', duration: parsed.value, unit: parsed.unit },\n            });\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (mappings.length === 0) return DEFAULT_FIELD_VALUES;\n    return { mappings };\n}\n\nexport default function CriterionCard({",
```

```
     1→// Relation configuration and constants
     2→
     3→import type {
     4→    FieldMapping,
     5→    FieldValue,
     6→    RelationCategory,
     7→    RelationOperator,
     8→    StructuredFieldFormValues,
     9→    TemporalUnit,
    10→} from './types';
    11→
    12→export const RELATIONS: Array<{
    13→    operator: RelationOperator;
    14→    label: string;
    15→    category: RelationCategory;
    16→}> = [
    17→    { operator: '=', label: 'equals (=)', category: 'standard' },
    18→    { operator: '!=', label: 'not equals (!=)', category: 'standard' },
    19→    { operator: '>', label: 'greater than (>)', category: 'standard' },
    20→    { operator: '>=', label: 'greater or equal (>=)', category: 'standard' },
    21→    { operator: '<', label: 'less than (<)', category: 'standard' },
    22→    { operator: '<=', label: 'less or equal (<=)', category: 'standard' },
    23→    { operator: 'within', label: 'within (range)', category: 'range' },
    24→    { operator: 'not_in_last', label: 'not in last (temporal)', category: 'temporal' },
    25→    { operator: 'contains', label: 'contains', category: 'standard' },
    26→    { operator: 'not_contains', label: 'does not contain', category: 'standard' },
    27→];
    28→
    29→export const RELATION_CATEGORY_MAP: Record<RelationOperator, RelationCategory> = RELATIONS.reduce(
    30→    (acc, rel) => {
    31→        acc[rel.operator] = rel.category;
    32→        return acc;
    33→    },
    34→    {} as Record<RelationOperator, RelationCategory>
    35→);
    36→
    37→export const TEMPORAL_UNITS: Array<{ value: TemporalUnit; label: string }> = [
    38→    { value: 'days', label: 'Days' },
    39→    { value: 'weeks', label: 'Weeks' },
    40→    { value: 'months', label: 'Months' },
    41→    { value: 'years', label: 'Years' },
    42→];
    43→
    44→export const DEFAULT_MAPPING: FieldMapping = {
    45→    entity: '',
    46→    relation: '' as const,
    47→    value: { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' },
    48→};
    49→
    50→export const DEFAULT_FIELD_VALUES: StructuredFieldFormValues = {
    51→    mappings: [DEFAULT_MAPPING],
    52→};
    53→
    54→export function getDefaultValueForCategory(category: RelationCategory): FieldValue {
    55→    switch (category) {
    56→        case 'standard':
    57→            return { type: 'standard', value: '', unit: '' };
    58→        case 'range':
    59→            return { type: 'range', min: '', max: '', unit: '' };
    60→        case 'temporal':
    61→            return { type: 'temporal', duration: '', unit: 'days' };
    62→    }
    63→}
```
